   cempilum kallilum uruvaṅkaḷ amaikkappaṭṭaṉa; avvuruvaṅkaḷ
   pūcikkappaṭṭaṉa. avarukku viḻā naṭaipeṟṟatu. appar, campantar, cuntarar
   pōṉṟa ciṟappu aṭiyār uruvaṅkaḷ pala kōvilkaḷil eḻuntaruḷappeṟṟaṉa.
   cuntarar uruvac cilaiyōṭu avar maṉaiviyar uruvac cilaikaḷum, avvāṟē
   ciṟuttoṇṭar, avar maṉaiviyār, makaṉār uruvac cilaikaḷum cila kōvilkaḷil
   eḻuntaruḷappeṟṟaṉa. cila cepput tirumēṉikaḷukku aṭiyil, ”ulakāṇṭa
   mūrtti.” tattā, namarē kāṇ eṉṟa milāṭuṭaiyār.” eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa ciṟapput
   toṭarkaḷ kuṟikkappaṭṭaṉa.
   nāyaṉmār ōviyaṅkaḷ
   tañcaip periya kōvil karuvaṟaiyiṉ puṟac cuvaroṉṟil cuntararaic
   civaperumāṉ taṭuttāṭkoḷḷum varalāṟum, cuntarar yāṉaimītum, cēramāṉ
   kutirai mītum kayilaicellum kāṭciyum aḻakoḻukum ōviyaṅkaḷākat
   tikaḻkiṉṟaṉa. cuntararum cēramāṉum talaimuṭiyum tāṭiyum vaittiruttal
   paṇṭaikkāla mayiroppaṉaiyum, avarkaḷ palavakai aṇikaḷai aṇintiruntamai
   akkāla aṇivakaikaḷaiyum namakku naṉku viḷakkuvaṉavākum.
   nāyaṉmār ciṟpaṅkaḷ
   ovvoru nāyaṉār varalāṟṟilum uyir nāṭiyākavuḷḷa nikaḻcciyaic ciṟpamākak
   kuṟikkum vaḻakkam cōḻar kālattil uṇṭāyiṟṟu. mēlaik kaṭampūrc civaṉ
   kōviṟ karuvaṟaiyiṉ puṟaccuvarkaḷil ittakaiya ciṟpaṅkaḷaik kāṇalām.
   iraṇṭām irācarācaṉ kaṭṭiya irācarācēccarattil ellā aṭiyār
   nikaḻccikaḷaiyum oruṅkē kāṉalām. kaṇṇappar varalāṟu paṟṟiya ciṟpaṅkaḷ
   tañcaip periya kōvil naṭu maṇṭapac cuvaril pala kāṭcikaḷākak
   kāṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. caṇṭīca nāyaṉār civaperumāṉāl koṉṟai mālai
   aṇivikkappaṭṭuc caṇṭīcap patam koṭukkappeṟṟa kāṭciyē annāyaṉār
   varalāṟṟil ciṟappup pakutiyākum. ikkāṭciyai irācēntira cōḻaṉ kaṭṭiya
   kaṅkai koṇṭa cōḻēccarattil iṉṟum kaṇṭu kaḷikkalām. nāyaṉmār
   tirumēṉikaḷum, avartam varalāṟṟuc ciṟpaṅkaḷum potumakkaḷ pārvaiyiṟ
   paṭṭu avarkaḷaic civaneṟic celvarkaḷākka vēṇṭum eṉpatē cōḻa maṉṉarkaḷiṉ
   viruppamākum:
   nāyaṉmār viḻākkaḷ
   ceṅkāṭṭaṅkuṭiyil ciṟutoṇṭa nampi viḻāvum, cīkāḻiyil ñāṉacampantar
   viḻāvum tiruvārūril cuntarar viḻāvum, tiruvatikaiyil tirunāvukkaracar
   viḻāvum, ciṟappāka naṭaipeṟṟaṉa. ivvāṟē pala kōvilkaḷil avvattalattu
   nāyaṉār viḻākkaḷ naṉku naṭaipeṟṟaṉa. avai naṉku naṭaipeṟak kaṭṭaḷaikaḷ
   piṟappikkappaṭṭaṉa eṉṟu pala kalveṭṭukaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. peruṅkōvil viḻāk
   kālaṅkaḷil tiruttoṇṭat tokai mutaliya patikaṅkaḷum, mārkaḻit tiṅkaḷil
   tiruvempāvaiyum ōtappaṭṭu vantaṉa.
   nāyaṉmār peyarkaḷ
   cōḻar kālac caiva makkaḷ nāyaṉmārmītu aḷavukaṭanta paṟṟukkoṇṭiruntaṉar
   eṉpatai aṟivikka, avartam peyarkaḷait tam piḷḷaikaṭku
   vaḻaṅkiyatiliruntu aṟiyalām. ciṟuttoṇṭa nampi, veṇkāṭṭu naṅkai, paravai
   naṅkaiyār, tiruveṇkāṭṭu naṅkai, kaṇṇappa tēvar, aiyāṟṟaṭikaḷ,
   pukaḻttuṇaiyaṭikaḷ, kārivēḷār, kōṭpuli, ciṅkaṉ kaliyaṉ, kāmaṉ tāyaṉ,
   ārūraṉ kampaṉ, nampiviṭaṅkaṉ, aṇukka vaṉṟoṇṭaṉ, uṭaiya nampi, kalaiyaṉ
   kumaraṉ, ālāla cuntarap pallavaraiyaṉ, taṇṭiyaṭikaḷ, kampaṉ
   maturāntakaṉ, tirunīlakaṇṭaṉ, iḷaiyāṉkuṭi kiḻavaṉ, kaliyaṉ maṉṟāṭi,
   paracamayakōḷari, māṉi, kalaiyaṉ māṇikkam pōṉṟa eṇṇiṟanta peyarkaḷ
   kalveṭṭukkaḷil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
   akkāla caiva makkaḷ nāyaṉmār peyarkaḷait tam piḷḷaikaṭku vaḻaṅkiyatōṭu
   maṭṭum nillātu, iṭaṅkaḷukkum apperumāṉkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷai iṭṭu vaḻaṅkiṉar
   eṉpatu kalveṭṭukkaḷ uṇarttum ceytiyākum. kaṇṇappa tēvar kamukantōppu,
   tiruñāṉa campanta nallūr, tiruñāṉacampanta maṅkalam eṉṉum iṭappeyarkaḷ
   iṅkuk kuṟikkattakkavai. pala maṭaṅkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ nāyaṉmār
   peyarkaḷaittāṅkiyiruntaṉa. parañcōti maṭam, tiruvarakīcaṉ maṭam,
   tillaivāḻ antaṇar maṭam, nampi tirumurukaṉ tirumaṭam, naminantiyaṭikaḷ
   maṭam, paracamayakōḷari maṭam, tiruttoṇṭat tokaiyāṉ tirumaṭam,
   tirumūlatēvar maṭam, ciṟuttoṇṭar tirumaṭam eṉpaṉa kuṟikkattakkavai.
   tirumuṟaikaḷum makkaḷum
   akkāla makkaḷ tirumuṟaikaḷiṭam koṇṭirunta aḷappariya paṟṟaip piṉvarum
   makkaṭpeyarkaḷum, ūrp peyarkaḷum naṉkuṇarttum. tēvāram aḻakiyāṉ, āṇṭār
   tiruttoṇṭat tokaiyāṉ, tirumuṟait tēvārac celvaṉ, tiruttoṇṭait tokai
   nallūr, tiruttoṇṭattokai maṅkalam eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa peyarkaḷaik kāṇka.
   tirumuṟaikaḷai naṉku paṭitta paḻakkattāl akkālac caiva naṉmakkaḷ
   nāyaṉmār payaṉpaṭuttiya coṟṟoṭarkaḷai makkaḷukkum ūrkaḷukkum
   piṟavaṟṟiṟkum peyarkaḷāka vaḻaṅkip pārāṭṭi makiḻntaṉar eṉpatum pala
   kalveṭṭukkaḷāl aṟiyakkiṭakkiṉṟatu. avaṟṟuḷ cila kīḻē kāṇka:
   (1) civāya "namaveṉṟu nīṟaṇintēṉ" eṉpatu appar tēvāram. "nīṟaṇintāṉ
   cētirāyaṉ" eṉpatu oru ciṟṟaracaṉ peyar. (2) "naccuvārkkiṉiyar pōlum
   nākaiyīc cuvaraṉārē" eṉpatu appar vākku. "nacciṉārk kiṉiyaṉ
   tillaiyampala mūvēnta vēḷāṉ" eṉpatu ōr araciyal aluvalaṉ peyar. (3)
   "maḻapāṭi vayirattūṇē eṉṟeṉṟē nāṉ araṟṟi" eṉpatu apparvācakam.
   "āṉaimaṅkalamuṭaiyāṉ pañcanati vayirattūṇ" eṉpatu oruvaṉ peyar. (4)
   aracāḷvar "āṇainamatai" eṉpatu campantar vākku. "āṇainamate"ṉṟa perumāṉ
   eṉpatu oruvaṉ peyar. (5) "poṉṉār mēṉiyaṉai" eṉpatu cuttarar coṟṟoṭar.
   "poṉṉār mēṉi viḷākam" eṉpatu ōriṭattiṉ peyar. (6) kōpperuñciṅkaṉ
   kālattil (ki. pi. 13-ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ muṟpātiyil) tiruveṇṇey nallūrk
   kōvilil, "piccaṉ eṉṟu pāṭac coṉṉāṉ" eṉṟa peyar koṇṭa ūtukompukaḷ iraṇṭu
   iruntaṉa.
   camaya nūl viḷakkam
   pala kōvilkaḷil caivacamaya nūlkaḷ paṭittup potumakkaḷukku viḷakkap
   paṭṭaṉa. pāratam irāmāyaṇam, civatarmam, tiruñāṉam, cōma cittāntam
   eṉpaṉa pala kōvilkaḷil pēcappaṭṭu vantaṉa. āḷuṭaiya nampi śrīpurāṇam
   (periya purāṇam) tiruvoṟṟiyūril paṭikkap paṭṭatu. ataṉaic cōḻaṉ iraṇṭām
   irācātirācaṉ iruntu kēṭṭu makiḻntāṉ eṉṟu tiruvoṟṟiyūrk kalveṭṭuk
   kuṟikkiṉṟatu. tirunīṭūril 'purāṇa nūl virikkum puricai māḷikai' oṉṟu
   mutaṟ kulōttuṅkaṉ kālattil iruntatākat terikiṉṟatu.
   āṭalum pāṭalum
   kōvilkaḷil camayat toṭarpāṉa āṭalkaḷaiyum pāṭalkaḷaiyum nikaḻtta āṭal
   pāṭalkaḷil valla makaḷir amarttappaṭṭiruntaṉar eṉpatai eṇṇiṟanta
   kalveṭṭukaḷ terivikkiṉṟaṉa. irācarācacōḻaṉ tañcaiyil kaṭṭiya periya
   kōvilil maṭṭum 400 kalaivāṇikaḷai amarttiyiruntāṉ. avarkaḷ tamiḻakattup
   palvēṟu kōvilkaḷiliruntu varuvikkap paṭṭavarkaḷ. ōvvoruttikkum oru vēli
   nilamum oru vīṭum vaḻaṅkap paṭṭaṉa. ivvāṟu pala kōvilkaḷil āṭal
   makaḷirum pāṭal makaḷirum iruntu camayap paṇiyāṟṟiṉar. ivaruḷ
   'patiyilār' oruvakaiyiṉar : 'riṣapat taḷiyilār' (civaṉ kōviṟ peṇṭukaḷ)
   maṟṟoru vakaiyiṉar ; tiruvoṟṟiyūrkkōvilil cokkam, cantikkuṇippam eṉṉum
   naṭaṉa vakaikaḷaip patiyilār āṭumpōtu, riṣapattaḷiyilār miḻaṟṟup
   pāṭalai vaḻaṅkiṉar. riṣapattaḷiyilār akamārkkam, varikkōlam eṉṉum
   naṭaṉa vakaikaḷai naṭittuk kāṭṭiṉar. patiyilār tiruppatikak
   karuttukaṭkum, tiruvempāvaik karuttukaṭkum ēṟṟavāṟu naṭittaṉar eṉpatu
   tiruvoṟṟiyūrk kalveṭṭāl terikiṉṟatu. ittakaiya tirup patikattiṟku ēṟṟa
   naṭaṉa vakaikaḷ iṉṟu illā tiruttal varuntaṟkuriyatu.
   tēvaraṭiyār
   kōvilkaḷiliruntu toṇṭāṟṟiya mātarkaḷuḷ tēvaraṭiyār oru vakaiyiṉar.
   ivarkaḷ kōvilil tiruvalakiṭal, tirumeḻukkiṭal, tiruvamutukkuriya
   ariciyait tūymai ceytal, tiruppatikam pāṭal mutaliya paṇikaḷaic ceytu
   vantaṉar; tirunīṟṟuk kāpputtaṭṭum malarttaṭṭum viḻākkaḷil ēnti
   vantaṉar; viḻāk kālaṅkaḷil ammaṉukkuk kavarivīciṉar; acceyalāl,
   'kavarippiṇā' eṉṟum aḻaikkappaṭṭaṉar. iṅṅaṉam tēvaraṭiyār nilaiyai
   aṭainta peṇkaḷ oru kuṟippiṭṭa cātiyarallar; ellā iṉattu makaḷirum ic
   caivappiṇiyai mēṟkoṇṭu cūla muttirai poṟikkap peṟṟut 'tēvaraṭiyār'
   eṉṉum takutiyaip peṟṟuvantaṉar. aḻakiya pāṇṭiya pallavaraiyaṉ eṉṟa
   paṭaittalaivaṉoruvaṉ taṉ kuṭumpap peṇkaḷait tēvaraṭiyār ākkiṉāṉ eṉṟu
   tiruvallam kōviṟ kalveṭṭut terivittalai nōkka, ivvuṇmai iṉitu
   pulaṉākiṉṟatu.
   maṭaṅkaḷ
   pautta matattiṟkum, camaṇa matattiṟkum accamayak kōvilkaḷai aṭuttu
   maṭaṅkaḷ iruntatupōlavē, caiva camaya vaḷarccikkuk kōvil kaḷai aṭuttum,
   taṉittum tamiḻakattil pala maṭaṅ kaḷ iruntaṉa. appar, campantar kāla
   mutalē maṭaṅkaḷ civappaṇiyaic ceytuvantaṉavāyiṉum cōḻar kālattil mikap
   pala maṭaṅkaḷ tōṉṟic camayat toṇṭiṉai naṉku āṟṟalāyiṉa. tiruvā
   vaṭutuṟaiyil maṭṭum tirunīla viṭaṅkaṉ maṭam, civalōka nāyakaṉ maṭam,
   carvatēvaṉ maṭam, pañcanativāṇaṉ maṭam, caṅkaratēvaṉ aṟaccālai,
   piramāṇa vācakaṉ maṭam, nāṟpatteṇṇāyiravaṉ maṭam, munnūṟṟirupattu
   nālvaṉ maṭam, perun tiruvāṭṭi aṟaccālai eṉpaṉa iruntaṉa. ivvāṟu pala
   peruṅ kōvilkaḷai aṭuttirunta maṭaṅkaḷukkup pakkattil maruttuva
   maṉaikaḷum amaippuṇṭiruntaṉa.
   ivaṟṟuḷ kālāmukac caivarkaḷiṉ maṭaṅkaḷ cila; kāpālikac caivar maṭaṅkaḷ
   cila; vīra caiva maṭaṅkaḷ mikaccila; vaṭanāṭṭuk kōḷaki maṭattaic cērnta
   kiḷai maṭaṅkaḷ cila; vāraṇācik kollā maṭattuk kiḷaikaḷ cila tamiḻakattu
   iruntaṉa; vāraṇāci pikṣā maṭattuk kiḷai maṭaṅkaḷ cilavum iruntaṉa. ivai
   aṉaittilum mēṟcoṉṉa pala vakaic camaya nūlkaḷ potumakkaḷukkup paṭittu
   viḷakkappaṭṭaṉa.
   tamiḻc caiva maṭaṅkaḷ
   māḷikai maṭattu mutaliyār cantāṉattaic cērnta maṭaṅkaḷ cila;
   caṇpaikkuṭi mutaliyār cantāṉa maṭaṅkaḷ cila; marutap perumāḷ cantāṉa
   maṭaṅkaḷ cila; ic caiva maṭattut talaivarkaḷ caiva cittānta
   cāttiraṅkaḷilum, caivat tiru muṟaikaḷilum ciṟantu viḷaṅkiṉar; makkaḷai
   nal vaḻippaṭuttiṉar. ic caiva maṭātipatikaḷiṉ celvākkut tamiḻakattil
   mikuntu paraviyatu. tamiḻakattuc caivam cātipētamaṟṟatu; 'aṉpē civam'
   eṉṟa koḷkaiyāl aṉaivaraiyum oru tāy īṉṟa makkaḷākak karutum virinta
   maṉap pāṉmaiyuṭaiyatu. ātalāl, perumpālarāṉa tamiḻ makkaḷ ic caiva
   maṭattut talaivarkaḷiṉ aruḷuraikaḷil peritum īṭupaṭṭaṉar. iccaiva
   maṭaṅkaḷaippaṟṟi ārāycciyāḷar piṉvarumāṟu kūṟutal kāṇka.*
   -----------------
   *......Saivism thus appears to have gradually grown stronger and
   stronger under the patronage of the *chola kings so that in the
   beginning of the 13th century we notice a number of mathas presided
   over by Saiva sanyasins, spreading their infludence over a pretty large
   portion of the Tamil country. All this must have been in spite of the
   teachings of the great Advita philosopher Sankaracharya whosedoctrines
   are well known. The philosophical expositions of Advaita teacher do not
   exclude the methods of pious devotion to be practised in the first
   stages of spiritual development, but insist on strict adherence and the
   paths of Karma as laid down in the sastras. The Saiva creed, on the
   other hand, does not appear to have paid much attention to sastric
   Karma but, taking unsullied devotion to Siva as its basis, it received
   into its fold all classes of people without any distinction of caste.
   ThisCatholicity of the Saiva faith rendered it not very popular with
   the orthodox Brahmanas but the non-brahman classes eagerly took to it
   and at the begining of the 13th century, as stated already, it was
   apparently very influential with its centres in many places...... The
   mathas of the Saiva sanyasins called Sivayogins (177 of 1908) of
   Maheswaras (164 of 1908) were mostly named after the famous Brahman
   Saiva saint Tirugnanasambandan and the non-Brahman saint
   Tirunavukarasar - A. R. E. 1909, 103.
   iṅṅaṉam cōḻap pēraracar ātaravil caiva camayam paṭippaṭiyāka
   vaḷarcciyuṟṟuk ki.pi.13 ām nūṟ ṟāṇṭiṉ toṭakkattil pala maṭaṅkaḷait
   tamiḻnāṭeṅkum peṟṟiruntatu. anta maṭaṅkaḷiṉ talaivarkaḷāka irunta
   caivat tuṟavikaḷ, perumpālāṉa tamiḻ makkaḷiṭam celvākkup peṟṟiruntaṉar.
   pukaḻ peṟṟa atvaita āciriyarāṉa caṅkarāccāriyār itē tamiḻakat tiliruntu
   pakti neṟiyaip parappiyavar. āṉāl, avar cāttiraṅkaḷil kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷapaṭi
   makkaḷ tattamakkuriya toḻilkaḷaic ceyyavēṇṭumeṉṟu vaṟpuṟuttiṉār.
   ivvaṟivuraikku māṟākac caiva camayam kāṇappaṭṭatu; cāti vēṟupāṭṭaik
   karutātu, ellā makkaḷaiyum pakti oṉṟaiyē aṭippaṭaiyākakkoṇṭu, taṉpāl
   aṇaittuk koṇṭatu. itaṉāl vaitikap pirāmaṇar caivattiṟ
   kalakkamuṭiyavillai; āyiṉ, pirāmaṇarallāta vakuppāraṉaivarum caivattait
   taḻuviṉar. itaṉ payaṉāka, muṉ coṉṉavāṟu caiva maṭaṅkaḷ nāṭeṅkum
   viḷaṅkiṉa. avai, pirāmaṇarāṉa tiru ñāṉacampantar peyarait tāṅkiyum ap
   pirāmaṇarāṉa tirunāvukkaracar peyarait tāṅkiyum mikappalavāka
   viḷaṅkiṉa.
   cōḻarkkup piṟpaṭṭa nāyakkar kālattil putuccēriyai aṭutta villiyaṉūril
   meykaṇṭa cantatimaṭam oṉṟiruntatu. immeykaṇṭa cantatiyil vanta
   namacivāya tēcikarāl ēṟpaṭuttappaṭṭatē tiruvāvaṭuturai ātīṉam. atē
   cantatiyaic cērnta ñāṉacampanta tēcikarāl uṇṭāṉatu tarumapura ātīṉam.
   caiva maṭaṅkaḷ ēṟattāḻak ki. pi. 10ām nūṟṟāṇṭiliruntu kōvil āṭciyaiyum,
   camaya vaḷarcciyaiyum kavaṉittu vantaṉa. ivai camaya yāttirikarkaḷukkup
   pukaliṭamāy viḷaṅkiṉa; camayak kalviyaiyum camayavoḻukkattaiyum
   potumakkaḷiṭaiyē vaḷarttaṉa. piṇiyāḷarkaḷum immaṭaṅkaḷil maruntu
   mutaliyaṉa peṟṟaṉar.
   kukaikaḷ
   ippeyar koṇṭa oruvakai maṭaṅkaḷ piṟkālac cōḻar kālattil vaḷarcci
   peṟṟaṉa. cīkāḻi, citamparam, tiruppukalūr mutaliya palavūrk kōvilkaḷil
   ivaiyiruntaṉa. ivai pātukāppu mikkavai; tirumuṟaikaḷil valla caivat
   tuṟavikaḷait talaivarkaḷākap peṟṟavai. ittalaivartam cīṭarkaḷ pala
   kōvilkaḷiliruntu tirumuṟaikaḷai ōtiṉārkaḷ. cila kukaikaḷil tavacikaḷ,
   āṇṭārkaḷ, yāttirīkar mutaliyōr uṇpikkappaṭṭaṉar. ittakaiya kukaikaḷālum
   muṉcollappaṭṭa maṭaṅkaḷālum caivacamayam naṉku pātukākkappaṭṭu
   vaḷarkkappaṭṭu vantatu eṉpatu kalveṭṭukkaḷāl aṟiyappaṭum uṇmaiyākum.
   makkaḷ toṇṭē camayattoṇṭu
   peruṅkōvilkaḷil potumakkaḷ nalaṉukkāka nūl nilaiyam iruntu vantatu.
   ataṉaic 'caracuvati paṇṭāram' eṉa vaḻaṅkiṉar. cila periya kōvilkaḷai
   aṭuttu maruttuvamaṉaikaḷ iruntaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ maruttuvarum (Physician),
   calliyakkiriyai paṇṇuvārum (Surgeon), tātimārum (Nurses), maruntup
   poruḷkaḷaik koṇṭu varupavarum, maruntukaḷaic ceyvōrum iruntaṉar.
   nōyāḷikaḷukkup paṭukkaikaḷ iruntaṉa.. ūril perum pañcam ēṟpaṭṭuk
   kuṭimakkaḷ tavikkumpōtu, avvūrk kōvilil uḷḷa veḷḷi, poṉ nakaikaḷaiyum
   pāttiraṅkaḷaiyum urukki ūrārkkuk kaṭaṉāka utavuvatum, pañcam nīṅkiya
   piṟaku utaviya kaṭaṉai mīṭṭup peṟuvatum vaḻakkamāka iruntatu eṉpatu
   ālaṅkuṭik kalveṭṭāl terikiṉṟatu.
   iṅṅaṉam kōvilkaḷ poruḷātārat tuṟaiyilum, uṭalnalat tuṟaiyilum, aṟivut
   tuṟaiyilum, camayat tuṟaiyilum potu makkaḷukkut toṇṭu
   ceytuvantamaiyāṟṟāṉ, tamiḻakattil caiva camayam makkaḷukkup
   payaṉpaṭattakka camayamāka viḷaṅkiyatu. ippaṇṭai nilai mīṇṭum
   putuppikkappaṭumāyiṉ, caiva camayam makkaḷ camayamāka malarccipeṟum.
   tirukkōvil vaḻipāṭum tāy moḻiyil naṭaipeṟutal vēṇṭum. tiruvoṟṟiyūrk
   kalveṭṭil kaṇṭapaṭi, tiruppatikaṅkaḷ, tiruvācakam ivaṟṟiṉ
   karuttukkaḷaip pulappaṭuttum naṭaṉa vakaikaḷum vaḷarcci peṟutal vēṇṭum.
   tamiḻ naṭaṉa āciriyarkaḷum, tamiḻ naṭaṉap peṇmaṇikaḷum ittuṟaiyil
   viraintu kavaṉañ celutta vēṇṭum.
   innilai ovvoru kōvililum ēṟpaṭumāyiṉ, nam tirumuṟaikaḷiṉ uṇmaic
   ciṟappiṉai makkaḷ uṇarntu payaṉpeṟutal kūṭum. im muṟaiyaip piṉpaṟṟāmal,
   tamiḻ makkaḷukkē puriyāta moḻiyil tamiḻakattuk kōvilkaḷil vaḻipāṭu
   naṭaipeṟuvatāl, maṉa vorumaippāṭṭukkuriya tirukkōvilkaḷ appaṇiyaic
   ceyyat tavaṟiyavai ākiṉṟaṉa. ātalāl nāṭṭiluḷḷa caiva naṉmakkaḷum,
   makkaḷ nalaṉukkāka viḷaṅkum aracāṅkamum ittuṟaiyil kavaṉañ celutti,
   tamiḻakattut tirukkōvilkaḷil tamiḻ vaḻipāṭṭiṉai naṭaimuṟaiyil koṇartal
   vēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉam tamiḻil vaḻipāṭu naṭaipeṟum naṉṉāḷē nam caivam
   taḻaittōṅkum poṉṉāḷākum.
   --------------------------

                       7. caivat tirumuṟaikaḷ - 1

   tirumantiram
   paṉṉiru tirumuṟaikaḷ
   tiruñāṉa campantar pāṭiya tirumuṟaikaḷ mūṉṟu; tirunāvukkaracar pāṭiya
   tirumuṟaikaḷ mūṉṟu; cuntarar pāṭiya tirumuṟai oṉṟu; maṇivācakar pāṭiya
   tiruvācakamum tirukkōvaiyārum eṭṭām tirumuṟai; tirumāḷikait tēvar,
   cēntaṉār, karuvūrttēvar, pūnturutti nampikāṭa nampi, kaṇṭarātittar,
   vēṇāṭṭu aṭikaḷ, tiruvāli amutaṉār, puruṣōttama nampi, cētirāyar eṉṟa
   oṉpatu aṭikaḷārkaḷ pāṭiya pāṭalkaḷ oṉpatām tirumuṟaiyil iṭampeṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
   tirumantiram pattām tirumuṟai; kāraikkālammaiyār mutaliya paṉṉiruvar
   pāṭiya pākkaḷiṉ tokuti patiṉōrām tirumuṟaiyākum. cuntarar pāṭiya
   tiruttoṇṭat tokaiyaip piṉpaṟṟiyum, nampiyāṇṭār nampi pāṭiya tiruttoṇṭar
   tiruvantātiyaip piṉpaṟṟiyum cēkkiḻāl pāṭiya periya purāṇam paṉṉiraṇṭām
   tirumuṟaiyākum.
   campantarum apparum ki. pi. 7-ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉar ātalāl mutal āṟu
   tirumuṟaikaḷ ki. pi. 7-ām nūṟṟāṇṭaic cērntavai. cuntararum
   māṇikkavācakarum ki. pi. 9-ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉar ātalāl ēḻām tirumuṟaiyum
   eṭṭām tirumuṟaiyum oṉpatām tirumuṟaiyum oṉpatām nūṟṟāṇṭaic cērntavai.
   pattām tirumuṟaiyākiya tirumantiram ki. pi. 4, 5, 6 ākiya mūṉṟu
   nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷil oru nūṟṟāṇṭaic cērntatu. patiṉōrām tirumuṟaiyiṟ kaṇṭa
   kāraikkālammaiyārum aiyaṭikaḷum campantarkku muṟpaṭṭavar. kallāṭar,
   kapilatēvar, paraṇatēvar, nakkīratēvar, cēramāṉ perumāḷ nāyaṉār eṉpavar
   ki. pi. 9-ām nūṟṟāṇṭukkuṭpaṭṭavar. nampiyāṇṭār nampi mutalām irācarācaṉ
   kālattavar. paṭṭiṉattaṭikaḷ ki. pi. 10-ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉar. oṉpatām
   tirumuṟai pāṭiya karuvūrttēvar mutaliyōr cōḻar (ki. pi. 900-1200)
   kālattavar.
   tirumantiram
   tirumantiram caivat tirumuṟaikaḷil kālattāl muṟpaṭṭatu. itaṉaic ceyta
   tirumūlar kētāram, nēpāḷam, kāci, vintam, srīcailam ivaṟṟait
   taricittukkoṇṭu teṉṉāṭuvantavar, vaṭamoḻi ākamaṅkaḷiṉ cattupporuḷait
   tamiḻil pāṭavēṇṭum eṉṟa karuttiṉāl mūvāyiram ceyyuṭkaḷaik koṇṭa
   tirumantiram eṉṉum nūlaic ceytaṉareṉṟu cēkkiḻār kūṟukiṉṟār.
   tirumantiram oṉpatu pakutikaḷāka uḷḷatu. ovvoru pakutiyum oru tantiram
   eṉappaṭum. tirumantiram tirumūlar kālattilirunta caivacamaya nilaiyai
   uṇarat tuṇaipurivatākum.
   (1) mutal tantirattil yākkai nilaiyāmai, celvam nilaiyāmai, iḷamai
   nilaiyāmai, kollāmai, pulāl uṇṇāmai, kāma aṭakkam, antaṇar oḻukkam,
   aracaṉ kaṭamai, aṟañ ceytaliṉ ciṟappu, aṉpai vaḷarttal, piṟarkku utavi
   ceytal, kaṟṟavariṭamiruntum nūlkaḷiliruntum aṟivai vaḷarttal, maṉattai
   viruppu veṟuppukkaḷiṟ cellaviṭāmai mutaliya aṟivuraikaḷ
   tarappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
   (2) iraṇṭām tantirattil akattiyar teṉṉāṭu pōntatu, civaṉuṭaiya eṭṭu
   vīrac ceyalkaḷ, liṅkattiṉ tōṟṟam, takkaṉatu yākam, piraḷayam paṟṟiya
   purāṇak kataikaḷ mutaliyaṉa kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. paṭaittāl, kāttal,
   aḻittal, maṟaittal, aruḷal eṉṉum civaṉuṭaiya aintoḻilkaḷum, civaṉ -
   cakti viḷaiyāṭṭāl uṇṭāṉa uyirkaḷaip paṟṟiya vivaraṅkaḷum
   viḷakkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. civa nintai, aṭiyār nintai kūṭāteṉpatum, kōvilkaḷai
   aḻippatu tīteṉpatum, poṟaiyuṭaimai, periyārait tuṇaikkōṭal
   mutaliyaṉavum pēcappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
   (3) mūṉṟām tantiram muḻuvatum yōkattaip paṟṟiyatu. iyamam, niyamam,
   ācaṉam, pirāṇāyāmam, pirattiyāhāram, tāraṇam, tiyāṉam, camāti eṉṉum
   eṇvakai yōkamuṟaikaḷum avaṟṟāl aṭaiyum payaṉkaḷum viḷakkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
   ivaṟṟukkuppiṉ uṭalai vellutal mutaliya yōkanilaic ceytikaḷ
   kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
   (4) nāṉkām tantiram mantira cātaṉam paṟṟiyatu. ippakutiyil ajapā
   mantiram, pairavi mantiram mutaliyaṉa kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. tiruampala
   cakkaram, tiripura cakkaram, ēroḷic cakkaram, pairavac cakkaram,
   cāmpavi maṇṭalac cakkaram, puvaṉātipati cakkaram, navākṣari cakkaram
   eṉpavai paṟṟiya vivaraṅkaḷ tarappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
   (5) aintām tantirattil cutta caivam, acutta caivam, mārkka caivam,
   kaṭuñcutta caivam eṉpavai paṟṟiya vivaraṅkaḷ tarappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. piṉpu
   cariyai, kiriyai, yōkam, ñāṉam eṉpaṉa kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa; piṉṉarc
   caṉmārkkamum ataṉ pirivukaḷāṉa cakamārkkam (kaṭavuḷai naṇpaṉākak karuti
   naṭattal), catputra mārkkam (kaṭavuḷukku makaṉāka naṭattal),
   tācamārkkam (kaṭavuḷai āṇṭāṉākak karuti naṭattal) eṉpaṉavum
   viḷakkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. aṭuttu, cālōkam (iṟai ulakil vacittal), cāmīpyam
   (kaṭavuḷukku aṇmaiyil vacittal), cārūpyam (iṟaiyōṭu camanilai aṭaital),
   cāyucyam (iṟaiyōṭu kalattal) eṉpaṉa kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷa. kaṭavuḷ
   cakti-mantam, mantataram, tīviram, tīvirataram eṉṉum nāṉku muṟaikaḷil
   iṟaṅkutal kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. caivattiṟkup puṟampāṉa camayaṅkaḷ
   kaṇṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. uṭcamayaṅkaḷ ēṟkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
   (6) āṟām tantirattil uyir nāṭiyāka uḷḷavai civakuru taricaṉam, avaṉatu
   tiruvaṭippēṟu, ñāṉattiṉ poruḷ, teripavaṉ-teriyappaṭṭa poruḷ, tuṟavu,
   tavam, aruḷiḷaliruntu tōṉṟum aṟivu, paktikkup pakkuvamuṭaiyavar
   ilakkaṇam, pakkuvam aṭaiyātār ilakkaṇam,tirunīṟṟiṉ poruḷ eṉpavaiyākum.
   (7) ēḻām tantirattil āṟu ātāraṅkaḷ, aṇṭaliṅkam, piṇṭaliṅkam, catāciva
   liṅkam, ātmaliṅkam, ñāṉaliṅkam, civaliṅkam eṉṉum āṟu liṅkaṅkaḷ, camayac
   ciṟappup pōtaṉai, ātmāvil uḷḷa civaṉukkēṟṟa vaḻipāṭu, kuruvākiya
   civaṉukkēṟṟa vaḻipāṭu, makēcuvara pūcai (aṭiyārkku uṇavaḷittal), aṭiyār
   perumai, uṇavu viṭuti, palavakai muttiraikaḷ, palavakai yōkikaḷ
   iṟappukkup piṉ aṭaiyattakum ulakaṅkaḷ, avarkaḷ taṅkaḷ uṭalaṅkaḷai viṭum
   muṟai, iṭakalai-piṅkalai nāṭikaḷ, pirāṇaṉ-puruṣaṉ-aṇu, cīvaṉ, pacu,
   pōtam eṉṉum āṟu, aimpulaṉkaḷai aṭakkum muṟai, kuruviṉ pōtaṉai, kūṭā
   oḻukkam mutaliyaṉ pēcappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
   (8) eṭṭām tantirattil uṭaliṉ amaippu, civattil kalakka uṭampai viṭum
   muṟai, patiṉoru avattaikaḷ, aṟivut tōṟṟam, cittāntattiṉ viḷakkam, piṟa
   camayaṅkaḷum ataṟkum uḷḷa toṭarpu, pati-pacu-pāca viḷakkam, piramaṉum
   mālum kāṇāta civaṉ eṉpataṟkuriya tattuva viḷakkam,
   kāmam-vekuḷi-mayakkam eṉṉum mūṉṟu kuṟṟaṅkaḷ, tat-tvam-aci eṉṉum mūṉṟu
   pataṅkaḷ, mūṉṟu turiyaṅkaḷ, mūṉṟu muktikaḷ, mūṉṟu corūpaṅkaḷ, mūṉṟu
   karaṇaṅkaḷ, mūṉṟu cūṉyaṅkaḷ, kāriya-kāraṇa upātikaḷ, upacāntam,
   puṟaṅkūṟāmai, civanintai oḻippu, akattāmarai paṟṟiya varuṇaṉai,
   'tattuvamaci, akam pirammāci' eṉpavai paṟṟiya ārāycci, uṇmai pēcal,
   ācaiyai oḻittal, pakti vaḷarttal, tūymai aṭaivatilum, viṭutalai
   peṟuvatilum nāṭṭam koḷḷutal mutaliyaṉa kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
   (9) oṉpatām tantirattil kuru, kurumaṭam, kuru taricaṉam
   mutaliyaṉapēcappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa; civāṉanta naṭaṉam, cuntara naṭaṉam,
   poṉṉampala naṭaṉam, poṉtillai naṭaṉam, āccariya naṭaṉam eṉpaṉa
   viḷakkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa; aṟivu malartal, uṇmai aṟiviṉ iṉpam, taṉ urukkāṭci,
   ūḻ, civaṉaik kaṇṭu avaṉatu uṇmai uruvai uṇartal. camāti nilai aṭaital
   mutaliyaṉa kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. iṟaivaṉaip paṟṟiya vaḻipāṭṭup
   pāṭalkaḷuṭaṉ innūl muṭivupeṟukiṉṟatu. tirumūlar niyāyam, vaicēṣikam,
   cāṅkiyam, yōkam, mīmāmcai, vētāntam eṉa vētaṅkaḷiliruntu kiḷaitta āṟu
   uṭpirivukaḷaik kūṟukiṉṟār.
   aṟuvakaic cittāntam
   pācupatam, māviratam, kāpālam, vā*mam, pairavam, caivam eṉpaṉa
   caivattiṉ uṭpirivukaḷākum.
   (1) pācupatam: āṉmākkaḷukku āṇava malam illai; māyai, kaṉmam eṉṟa
   iraṇṭāl panta muṟṟu iṉpa tuṉpaṅkaḷai nukarum. ivaṟṟil veṟuppuṟṟuc
   cāttira muṟaiyāl tīṭcai peṟṟa vaṉiṭam iṟai aṟivu paṟṟum. appoḻutu
   iṟaivaṉ taṉ kuṇaṅkaḷai avaṉpāl paṟṟuvit tut taṉ atikārattiliruntu
   ōyvupeṟuvāṉ. itu pācupatar koḷkai. pācupataṉ taṉ uṭam pil cāmpalaiyum
   kaḷimaṇṇaiyum pūcuvaṉ; kaṟuppu uṭaikaḷai aṇivaṉ; talaiyai muṭiyiṭṭuk
   koḷ vaṉ; muṭiyiṭāmalum viṭuvaṉ. pācupatar itikāca kālamutalē nāṭṭil
   iruppavar; pacupati vaḻipāṭ ṭiṉar; liṅka vaḻipāṭṭiṉar; civaṉuṭaṉ
   kaṇaṅkaḷai yum pēykaḷaiyum iṇaittu vaḻipaṭuvar; cāti vēṟu pāṭaṟṟavar;
   cepam, tavam ivaṟṟil īṭupaṭṭavar; mukam, kaikaḷ, mārpu, koppūḻ ivaṟṟil
   liṅka muttirai patippavar. civaṉ yōka ācāriyaṉāka vantu kuru, tatīci,
   akattiyaṉ, upamaṉyu eṉṟa nālvarkkum pācupata ñāṉattai upatēcittāṉ.
   avarkaḷ pācupata ñāṉattaip palarkkum upatē cittaṉar eṉpatu pācupatar
   nampikkai. cariyai, kiriyai, yōkam, ñāṉam eṉpaṉa iccaivattil
   vaṟpuṟuttappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
   2. māviratam: pācupatakkoḷkaiyil kūṟappaṭṭa āṉmākkaḷ pantamuṟṟu
   iṉpatuṉpaṅkaḷai nukarum. cāttirattil kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷa muṟaiyil tīṭcai
   peṟṟu elumpu mālai aṇital mutaliya cariyaikaḷil vaḻuvātu oḻukupavar
   muttarāvar. muttarukkuc civaṉōṭu camamāka ellāk kuṇaṅkaḷum
   uṟpattiyākum. itu māviratiyar koḷkai. ivarkaḷ neṟṟiyil mūṉṟu
   kīṟṟukkaḷākā vipūti aṇivar; uṭal muḻuvatu nīṟu pūcuvar; talaimayiriṉ
   ucciyil elumpu maṇikaḷ kaṭṭiyiruppar; kātil elumpu maṇikaḷaik
   kuṇṭalaṅkaḷāka aṇivar; kaḻuttil elumpu maṇit tāḻvaṭam aṇivar; muṉkaiyil
   elumpu maṇi kōtta kayiṟu kaṭṭuvar; tōḷil yōkap paṭṭikaiyum, kaṟuppu
   mayirk kaṟṟaiyālāṉa pūṇūlum aṇivar; kōvaṇam tarippar; ataṉmēl
   acaintāṭum ciṟiya uṭaiyait tarippar; maṇṭai ōṭṭil uṇavu koḷvar.
   cuṭukāṭṭuc cāmpalai uṭal muḻuvatum pūcuvar. ivaruḷ oru piriviṉar
   "kāḷāmukar" eṉṟum kūṟappaṭuvar.
   3. kāpālam: āṉmākkaḷiṉ iyalpu, panta iyalpu māviratiyar koṇṭavāṟē
   kāpālikaḷum koṇṭuḷḷaṉar. cāttira muṟaippaṭi tīṭcai peṟṟup paccaikkoṭi
   oṉṟaik kaiyil ēnti nāḷtōṟum maṉitar talaiyōṭṭil aiyam ēṟṟu uṇpavar,
   muttarāyc civaṉ āvēcittalāl ellāk kuṇaṅkaḷum peṟṟuc civacamayamāvar
   eṉpatu kāpālikar koḷkai. kāpālikam, pācupatampōlap paḻaimaiyāṉatu.
   kāpālikar civaṉaik koṭiya pairava vaṭivil vaṇaṅkuvar; uṭampil
   piṇaccāmpal pūcuvar; talaiyil caṭai uṭaiyavar; talai mālai aṇipavar;
   pulit tōlālāṉa kōvaṇattai uṭuppar; iṭakkaiyil kapālam ēntiyiruppar.
   valakkaiyil maṇi oṉṟu piṭittiruppar. ikkāpālikar narapaliyil
   īṭupaṭṭavar. ciṟut toṇṭar ippirivaic cērntavar ātalāl pairava vēṭattil
   vanta aṭiyavarukkut tam maintaṉai aṟuttuk kaṟi camaittup paṭaittār
   eṉpatu iṅku aṟiyattakkatu.
   4. vāmam - ulakam cakti mayamāṉatu. vāma nūlil vititta muṟaippaṭi
   oḻukic caktiyil layittalē mukti eṉpatu cakti vaḻipāṭṭiṉar koḷkai.
   ivvaḻipāṭṭiṉar 'cāktar' eṉap paṭuvar. mikappaḻaiya kālattil tōṉṟiya tāy
   vaḻipāṭṭiṉ vaḷarcciyē 'vāmam' allatu 'cāktēyam' eṉpatu.
   5. pairavam - perumpālum vāma matattōṭu ottuc ciṟupāṉmai ācāraṅkaḷil
   vēṟupaṭṭup pairavaṉē paramporuḷ eṉṟu nampi, pairava matattil cēruvatē
   mukti aḷikkum eṉpatu ic camayattiṉar kōṭpāṭu.
   6. caivam - civaṉaiyē muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷā kakkoṇṭu cariyai mutaliya
   nāṉkiṉālum avaṉai aṭaiyap pāṭupaṭum camayamē caivam. pati - pacu -
   pācam eṉṉum mūṉṟaiyum oppukkoṇṭu ākama vaḻippaṭi naṭappavar
   'mākēcuvarar' eṉappaṭuvar. vīracaivam - vīracaivar allatu vīramākēcu
   varar allatu liṅkāyattukaḷ eṉpavar civaṉai maṭṭumē liṅka vaṭivil
   vaḻipaṭuvar. pakti neṟi yaip piṉpaṟṟi oḻukkattōṭu vāḻkkai naṭatti
   liṅkattai vaḻipaṭuvōr tuṟakkam aṭaivar eṉ patu vīracaivar nampikkai.
   ivarkaḷ vaṭamoḻi vētaṅkaḷai oppukkoḷḷātavar; cāti vēṟupāṭu illātavar;
   kuḻantai maṇam ceyyātavar; kaim peṇ maṟumaṇam ceypavar; pulālaiyum
   kuṭiyaiyum oḻittavar; iṟantāraip putaippavar; liṅkattaip pōlak kullāy
   taittut talaiyil aṇipa varum ivaruṭ cilarāvar. vīra ākamam, vātuḷa
   ākamam eṉṉum iraṇṭē ivartam matippukkuri yavai. tirumūlar ivviru
   ākamaṅkaḷaiyum pārāṭṭiyuḷḷār. eṉavē, tirumūlar kālattil vīracaivam
   nāṭṭil iruntatu eṉpatu naṉku terikiṟatu aṉṟō?
   caivattiṉ nāṉku pirivukaḷ
   tirumūlar kālattuc caivam (1) cutta caivam, (2) acutta caivam, (3)
   mārkka caivam, (4) kaṭuñ cutta caivam eṉa nālvakaippaṭṭatu. ippirivu
   piṟkālac caiva cittānta cāttiraṅkaḷil illai eṉpatu kavaṉikkattakkatu.
   1. cutta caivar pati - pacu - pācam eṉpavaṟ ṟiṉ vēṟupāṭukaḷai
   uṇarntavar; 'cit' eṉṉum aṟivai, 'acit' eṉṉum avittaiyāl keṭāmaṟ
   pārppavar; tūya māyaiyuṭaṉ acutta māyaiyaik kalakka viṭātavar.
   vētattiliruntu tōṉṟiya cittāntamē cuttacaiva cittāntam eṉpatu tirumūlar
   karuttu.
   2. acutta caivar kātil kuṇṭalaṅkaḷai aṇivar; uṭampil nīṟu pūcuvar;
   ruttirāṭca mālaiyai aṇivar; cariyai, kiriyai vaḻi niṟpar; upatēcam
   peṟṟu mantiraṅkaḷaic cepittuk koṇṭiruppar.
   3. mārkka caivar kātil kuṇṭalaṅkaḷai aṇivar; uṭampil nīṟu pūcuvar;
   puṟavēṭattiṟku mikunta matipputtārār; ñāṉattaiyē, ciṟappākak karutuvar;
   vētāntattuṭaṉ kūṭiya cittāntap pātaiyiṟ ceṉṟu jīvaṉ muktarāvar.
   4. kaṭuñcutta caivar eṉpavar caivarkkuriya veḷi vēṭaṅkaḷil kavaṉam
   celuttār; nērē civaṉiṭam ceṉṟu taṅkaḷ pācattaiyum pacutvattaiyum
   oḻittuc civañāṉattai aṭaivar. avarkaḷ cutta caivarkkuriya
   camayappaṭikaḷiṟ cellār; avarkaḷ 'nāṉ', 'avaṉ' eṉṟa vēṟupāṭu
   aṟṟavarkaḷ; collariya tattuvattai aṭaintu intiriyaṅkaḷai
   aṭakkiyavarkaḷ. avarkaḷ paracāyutyattai aṭaintavarkaḷ. kaṇṇappar,
   cākkiyar pōṉṟa nāyaṉmārkaḷ palar iv vakuppaic cērntavarāvar. avarkaḷ
   cariyai, kiriyai, yōkam, ñāṉam eṉṉum paṭikaḷaik kaṭantavarkaḷ;
   puṟavēṭam aṟṟavarkaḷ. kaṭuñ cutta caivam civañāṉa pōtam mutaliya
   piṉṉūlkaḷil kūṟappaṭāmai kāṇattakkatu. kaṭuñcutta caivaruṭaiya
   ilakkaṇaṅkaḷait tirumūlar kūṟiyirāviṭil, nāyaṉmār palaruṭaiya ceyalkaḷ
   pittar ceyalkaḷākak kāṇappaṭṭirukkum; viḷakkamum matippum peṟṟirā; nām
   vēṟu enta nūlaik koṇṭum uṇmai aṟintirukka muṭiyātu.
   innālvaruḷ acutta caivar vētāntattuṭaṉ kūṭiya cittāntattai aṟiyātavar.
   vētāntattuṭaṉ kūṭiya cittāntam cātāraṇa caivar upāyam eṉṟu tirumūlar
   kūṟukiṟār. kaṭuñ cutta caivar ellām kaṭantavar. eṉavē, cuttacaivarum
   mārkka caivarumē vētāntattuṭaṉ kūṭiya cittāntattai aṟintavar eṉpatum,
   vētāntattuṭaṉ kūṭiya caiva cittāntamē 'cutta caivam' eṉpatu tirumūlar
   karuttātal kāṇka.
   caṉmārkkam mutaliya nāṉku
   caṉmārkkam - civaṉ tiruvaṭikaḷaik kāṇutal, toḻutal, niṉaital, toṭutal,
   pukaḻtal, talaiyilē cūṭutal, ivaṟṟaic ceypavar muktiyaṭaivar.
   cakamārkkam - eṉpatu iṟaivaṉaiyē naṇpaṉākak koṇṭu vaḻipaṭṭu
   muktiyaṭaiyum vaḻi. cuntarar piṉpaṟṟiya neṟi ituvē.
   catputramārkkam - eṉpatu civaṉait tantaiyākavum aṭiyavaṉ taṉṉaip
   piḷḷaiyākavum koṇṭu vaḻipaṭuvatu. civaṉaip pūcittal, tōttirap pārāyaṇam
   ceytal, pōṟṟutal, cepittal, uruva vaḻipāṭu ceytal mutaliyaṉa ittuṟaikku
   uriyavai. campantar piṉpaṟṟiya neṟi ituvēyākum.
   tācamārkkam - eṉpatu civaṉait talaivaṉākavum taṉṉai aṭimaiyākavum koṇṭu
   vaḻipaṭṭu muktiyaṭaital, viḷakkēṟṟal, malar koytal, alakiṭal,
   meḻukutal, maṇiyaṭittal, apiṭēka nīraik koṇartal mutaliya kōvil
   toḻilkaḷ ittuṟaikku uriyaṉa.
   civa pakti
   āṟām tantirattul civapakti naṉku viḷakkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. pācattiliruntu
   viṭupaṭṭu muttiyaṭaiya virumpum oruvaṉ pala paṭikaḷaik kaṭakkavēṇṭum.
   mutalil kuruparaṉ avaṉukku mukti vaḻikāṭṭuvāṉ; avvaḻiyē ceṉṟāl
   cuttakuru tōṉṟik kaṭavuḷ aruḷai nalkuvāṉ. annilaiyil paktaṉ
   mēṟcollappaṭṭa cittikaḷ yōka caktikaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟai aṭaikiṟāṉ. aṭutta
   nilaiyil cat-kuru tōṉṟip paktaṉuṭaiya āṇavam-māyai-kaṉmam eṉṉum
   mummalaṅkaḷai akaṟṟuvāṉ; muktikkuc celuttuvāṉ. piṉṉarc civa kuru
   kāṭciyaḷittut cat, acat, catacat eṉpavaṟṟaik kāṭṭuvāṉ. inta aṟivil āṉmā
   nilaittapoḻutu civamākiṟatu.
   civa atvaitam
   āṉmā 'tāṉ' vēṟu, 'avaṉ' vēṟu eṉṉum eṇṇattil irukkum varaiyil 'tvaitam'
   allatu 'irumai' irukkum. taṉippaṭṭa āṉmā avaṉil kalantuviṭumāyiṉ,
   irumai oḻintu 'orumai' ēṟpaṭum. itu 'civātvaitam' eṉappaṭum.
   caṉmārkka maṭaṅkaḷ ēḻu
   tirumūlar kālattil ēḻu maṭaṅkaḷ caṉmārkka caivattaip piṉpaṟṟi
   ēṟpaṭṭaṉa. ovvoṉṟilum oru kuru iruntār. mūlar (tirumūlar), kālāṅkar,
   akōrar, māḷikait tēvar, nātāntar, maramāṉantar, pōkatēvar eṉṉum eḻuvar
   maṭattuk kuḻuviṉar āvar. tirumūlar maṭattu vaḻi vanta cīṭarkaḷ "mūlaṉ
   marapiṉar" eṉappaṭṭaṉar; avarkaḷ pala nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷ tamiḻ nāṭṭil iruntu
   vantaṉar. avaruḷ oruvarē ki.pi. 17-ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉarāṉa tāyumāṉavarkkuk
   kuruvāka iruntavar. itaṉai,
   "mantrakuru vēyōka tantkuru vēmulaṉ
   marapil varu mauṉa kuruvē!"
   eṉṉum avar pāṭalaik koṇṭu aṟiyalām. tiruccirāppaḷḷi malaimītu uḷḷa
   mauṉa kuru maṭam inta mauṉa kuṭuvuṭaṉ toṭarpuṭaiyatu ākum.
   caivap piracāram
   tirumūlar tamatu nūlil caiva camayattiṉ ciṟappai viḷakkiyatuṭaṉ
   nillātu, camayap piracāram ceytiruppatum kāṇalām. "civaṉukku nikarākum
   teyvam vēṟillai, avaṉ piṟappu aṟṟavaṉ; pēraruḷāḷaṉ, yāvarkkum iṉpam
   aruḷvāṉ; avaṉait toḻuṅkaḷ; ñāṉam peṟalām; avaṉai enta vakaiyilum
   ēttalām. avaṉ aruḷ purivāṉ; kāraikkāl ammaiyārum paraṇa tēvarum
   kaiyāṇṭa muṟaippaṭiyē tiru mūlarum tam peyaraiyum, nūl paṭippatāl
   aṭaiyum payaṉaiyum kuṟittuḷḷār. ikkuṟippup palaraiyum payaṉ karutip
   paṭikkat tūṇṭavallatu.
   tirumantirac ciṟappu
   ākamāntam allatu caiva cittānta cāttiraṅkaḷ eṉpaṉa cumār ki.pi. 4
   allatu 5-ām nūṟṟāṇṭil ciṟappuṟṟaṉa eṉṉalām. kuptarkaḷ kālattil
   kōtāvariyāṟṟaṅkaraiyil mantra kāḷēcuvar eṉṟa iṭattil mantrakāḷēvacuvar
   kōvilaiccuṟṟi nāṉku caiva maṭaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa. aṅku ākamac caivarkaḷ
   iruntārkaḷ. inta 4,5,6-ām nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷil tirumūlar oru nūṟṟāṇṭaic
   cērntavarātal vēṇṭum eṉpatu muṉṉarē kūṟappaṭṭatu. tirumūlarukku muṉ
   tamiḻnāṭṭil tamiḻ moḻiyil caiva cittānta nūl illai, avar ceyta
   tirumantiramē caiva cittānta mutal nūl eṉpatu teḷivu. ātaliṉ innūl piṉ
   vanta appar, campantar ,cuntarar mutaliya camayācāriyarkaṭkum avarkaḷāl
   parappappaṭṭa caiva camayattiṟkum ātāra nūlāyiṟṟu. avarkaḷ
   tirumantirattai naṉku paṭittavarkaḷa eṉpataṟkuriya cāṉṟukaḷ pala
   kāṭṭalām. iṭam pōtāmaiyāl, cila cāṉṟukaḷ iṅkut taruvōm.
   ----------
   1. (1) "kuruvē civameṉak kūṟiṉaṉ nanti" -tirumantiram
   (2) "oruva rāyiru mūvaru māyavaṉ kuruva tāya kuḻakaṉ" -appar tēvāram
   2. (1) "cāttira mōtum caturkaḷai viṭṭunīr
   māttiraip pōtu maṟittuḷḷē nōkkumiṉ
   pārttaip pārvai pacumarat tāṇipōl
   ārtta piṟavi akalaviṭ ṭōṭumē" -tirumantiram
   itē aṟivuraiyai appar kūṟutal kāṇka:
   (2) "cātti rampala pēcum caḻakkarkāḷ
   kōtti ramuṅku lamuṅkoṇ ṭeṉceyvīr.
   pātiti rañciva meṉ paṇitirēl
   mātti raikkuḷ aruḷumāṟ pēṟarē"
   -appar tēvāram.
   (3) "vēyaṉa tōḷikku vēntoṉṟuntāṉē"
   eṉpatu tirumantiram.
   "vēyuṟutōḷipaṅkaṉ viṭamuṇṭakaṇṭaṉ"
   eṉpatu campantar tēvāram.
   (4) "attaṉ tiruvaṭik kappālaik kappālām"
   "appālaik kappālaip pāṭutuṅkāṇ ammāṉāy"
   eṉpatu tiruvācakam .
   (5) "eppari cāyiṉum ēttumiṉ ēttiṉāl
   appari cīcaṉ aruḷpeṟa lāmē"
   eṉpatu tirumantiram. ikkaruttu,
   "epperun taṉmaiyum evvevar tiṟamum
   appari cataṉāl āṇṭukoṇ ṭaruḷiyum"
   eṉavarum tiruvācaka aṭikaḷil kāṇka.
   -------------

                       8. caivattiru muṟaikaḷ. -II

   mutal mūṉṟu tirumuṟaikaḷ
   caiva camayat tirumuṟaikaḷ paṉṉiraṇṭu. mutal mūṉṟu pāṭiyavar campantar.
   ñāṉacampantar ēṟattāḻa 300 patikaṅkaḷaip pāṭiyuḷḷār. ovvoru patikamum
   pattu allatu patiṉoru pākkaḷaik koṇṭatu. patikaṅkaḷ akkāla
   vaḻakkilirunta intaḷam, kuṟiñci mutaliya paṇkaḷil pāṭappaṭṭaṉa. ovvoru
   patikattilum, appatikam pāṭappeṟṟa ūriṉ iyaṟkaiyamaippu, avvūrk kōvilil
   uḷḷa civaperumāṉiṉ ciṟappu, avaṉai aṭaivatāl peṟattakum payaṉ, irāvaṇaṉ
   kayilaiyaip peyarttup tuṉpappaṭṭamai, camaṇar-pauttar vacavu eṉpaṉa
   perumpālum iṭam peṟṟiruttal kāṇalām.
   "pōtiyarkaḷ piṇṭiyarkaḷ pōtuvaḻu
       vātavakai yuṇṭupalapoy
       ōtiyavar koṇṭuceyva toṉṟumilai
       naṉṟatuṇar vīruraimiṉō
       ātiyemai yāḷuṭaiya arivaiyoṭu
       pirivilia marntapatitāṉ
       cātimaṇi teṇṭiraiko ṇarntuvayal
       pukaeṟikoḷ caṇpainakarē"

   campantar pāṭalkaḷil talaṅkaḷiṉ iyaṟkai yaḻaku, pala varalāṟṟuc
   ceytikaḷ, icai, naṭaṉam, camaṇar pauttar paṟṟiya ceytikaḷ, tamakku
   muṉṉiruntavarum tam kālattavarumāṉa nāyaṉmār paṟṟiya kuṟippukkaḷ,
   palavakaic civaṉaṭiyāraippaṟṟiya kuṟippukkaḷ, tammuṭaiya vāḻkkai
   nikaḻccikaḷ, kōvilkaḷil naṭaipeṟṟa viḻākkaḷ, vaḻipāṭṭu muṟaikaḷ,
   purāṇakkataik kuṟippukkaḷ, caivacamayak kuṟippukkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟaik
   kāṇalām. talattiṉ iyaṟkaiyaḻakai viḷakkum pāṭalkaḷil ceṭikaḷ, koṭikaḷ,
   maraṅkaḷ, malarkaḷ yāvum kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷamai, campantaratu kavaṉikkum
   āṟṟalaiyum iyaṟkai aḻakai anu pavikkum maṉappaṇpaiyum naṉku viḷakkum.
   campantar kālattil cīkāḻi kaṭalarukil iruntatu; nākai ciṟanta
   tuṟaimukap paṭṭiṉamāka iruntatu, eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa varalāṟṟuk kuṟippukkaḷ
   pala avar tiruppatikaṅkaḷil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avar kālattil
   maṟaiyavarkaḷ mikutiyāka vāḻnta talaṅkaḷ evai eṉpataiyum vēṟupala potuk
   karuttukkaḷaiyum avar pākkaḷ terivikkiṉṟaṉa. avar kālattil pala ūrkaḷil
   irunta kōvilkaḷ taṉippeyar tāṅki iruntaṉa eṉpataiyum nām pala pākkaḷāl
   aṟiyalām. ceṅkāṭṭaṅ kuṭiyil kaṇapatīccaram eṉṟa kōvilum, āvūril
   pacupatīccaram eṉṉum kōvilum, naraiyūril cittīccaram eṉṟa kōvilum,
   mayilāppūril kapālīccaram eṉṟa kōvilum, ivvāṟē pala ūrkaḷul vēṟuvēṟu
   peyarkoṇṭa kōvilkaḷum iruntaṉa.
   appar tirumuṟaikaḷ
   appar pāṭalkaḷum ēṟattāḻa mūvāyirattukku mēṟpaṭṭavai. campantaraip
   pōlavē apparum tam kālattavarum tamakku muṟpaṭṭavarumāṉa nāyaṉmār ceyta
   tiruppaṇikaḷait tam pāṭalkaḷil kuṟittuḷḷār. ivar pākkaḷilum campantar
   pākkaḷiliruntu aṟiyappaṭum icai, naṭaṉam mutaliya palavakaic
   ceytikaḷaiyum nirampa aṟiyalām. mailāppūr ivar kālattil ciṟanta
   tuṟaimukappaṭṭiṉamāka iruntatu. pallava makēntiravarmaṉ icai, naṭaṉam,
   nāṭakam im mūṉṟaiyum vaḷarttavaṉātalāl avaṉ kālattil vāḻnta apparuṭaiya
   pāṭalkaḷil icai, naṭaṉam paṟṟiya kuṟippukkaḷ mikutiyākak
   kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. camaṇaraip paṟṟiya kuṟippukkaḷum ivar pāṭalkaḷil
   palavākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. campantaraip pōlavē ivar ūrppeyarum avvūrk
   kōviṟ peyarum putiyaṉavākap pala iṭaṅkaḷil kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār. cīkāḻik
   kōvilaip puḷḷiṉaṅkaḷ ēntiṉa katai pōṉṟa mikappala purāṇak kataikaḷ
   (Mythologies) ivar pāṭalkaḷil iṭam peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. irumputal, pērūr,
   tañcait taḷikkuḷam, iṭavai, kāmpīli, eḻumūr mutaliya pala
   vaipputtalaṅkaḷ ivarāl kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. paḷḷi eṉṟu muṭiyum
   civattalaṅkaḷum, vīraṭṭam, kuṭi, ūr, kāṭu, vāyil, malai, āṟu, kuḷam,
   kaḷam eṉa muṭiyum talaṅkaḷum ivarāl tokaippaṭuttik kuṟikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
   ip periyār cūlai nōyāl varuntiyatu mutaliya tam vāḻkkai nikaḻccikaḷ
   palavaṟṟait tam patikaṅkaḷiṟ pāṭiyuḷḷār; tāmkaṇṭa viḻākkaḷaip paṟṟiya
   vivaraṅkaḷaip pala patikaṅkaḷil kūṟiyuḷḷār. aṭiyār malarum nīrum koṇṭu
   kōvil cellutal, "vāḻi-pōṟṟi" eṉṟu iṟaivaṉai ēttutal, akanekiḻcciyōṭu
   kōvilukkuc cellutal, cilar pāṭikkoṇṭē kōvilukku varutal, āṭavarum
   peṇṭirum kōvilai valam varutal, pāṭutal, paṇital, mūṉṟu canti vaṇakkam
   mutaliya vaḻipāṭṭu muṟaikaḷ ivarāl naṉku viḷakkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
   "nilaipeṟumā ṟeṇṇutiyēl neñcē nīvā
       nittalumem pirāṉuṭaiya kōvil pukkup
       pularvataṉmuṉ alakiṭṭu meḻukku miṭṭup
       pūmālai puṉaintēttip pukaḻntu pāṭit
       talaiyārak kumpiṭṭuk kūttu māṭic
       caṅkarā cayapōṟṟi pōṟṟi yeṉṟum
       alaipuṉalcēr ceñcaṭaiyem āti yeṉṟum
       ārūrā eṉṟeṉṟē alaṟā nillē"

   eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa apparkūṟum aṟivuraip pāṭalkaḷ palavākum. avai paṭittu
   iṉpuṟaṟ pālaṉa.
   ēḻām tirumuṟai.
   ittirumuṟai ēṟattāḻa āyiram pāṭalkalaḷaik koṇṭatu. apparatu piracāra
   vaṉmaiyāl toṇṭaināṭu caiva nāṭāka māṟiyatu; campantaratu toṇṭiṉāl
   pāṇṭiyanāṭu civamaṇam kamaḻum nāṭāka māṟiyatu. ātalāl camaṇamum pautta
   mum vali kuṉṟiviṭṭaṉa. ataṉāl appar campantarkku ēṟattāḻa nūṟu āṇṭukaḷ
   kaḻittu vanta cuntararkkup puṟaccamaya etirpput tōṉṟavillai. ātaliṉ,
   avar pākkaḷil puṟaccamayak kaṇṭaṉam mikutiyāka illai eṉpatu
   kavaṉikkattakkatu. appar campantar ceṉṟa neṟiyaip piṉpaṟṟiyē
   ceṉṟavarātalāl cuntarar pākkaḷilum pala iṭaṅkaḷaippaṟṟiya varalāṟṟuk
   kuṟippukkaḷ mutaliyaṉa kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. āyiṉ, tam kāla varalāṟṟuk
   kuṟippukkaḷai mikutiyākat tarum ciṟappu ivarpāl
   kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.
   " kūṭā maṉṉaraik kūṭṭattu veṉṟa koṭiṟaṉ kōṭpuli"
       "maṇṇulakam kāval koṇṭa urimaiyāl pallavarkkut tiṟaikoṭā
       maṉṉavarai maṟukkañ ceyyum puliyūrcciṟṟampalattem perumāṉ"
       "kaṭalcūḻnta ulakamellām kākkiṉṟa perumāṉ
       kāṭavarkōṉ, kaḻaṟciṅkaṉ aṭiyārkkum aṭiyēṉ"

   eṉpaṉa pōṉṟavai varalāṟṟuc ciṟappuṭaiyaṉa.
   marukal nāṭṭu marukal, koṇṭal nāṭṭuk koṇṭal, miḻalai nāṭṭu miḻalai,
   veṇṇi nāṭṭu miḻalai eṉṟāṟ pōṉṟa toṭarkaḷ ivar kālattirunta nāṭṭup
   piriviṉaiyai naṉkuṇarttutalōṭu, ivaratu nāṭṭu aṟivaiyum naṉku
   viḷakkuvaṉavākum. ivar tam kālattuc cēra, cōḻa, pāṇṭiya, pallava vēntar
   nālvarkkum naṇparāka iruntār; tamiḻakam muḻumaiyum cuṟṟip pārkkum
   vāyppiṉaip peṟṟār. pala talaṅkaḷ irunta nilaiyaiyum, kōvilkaḷ
   ciṟappuṟṟirunta nilaiyaiyum ivar pāṭalkaḷ naṉku terivikkiṉṟaṉa. ivarāl
   kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷa vaipput talaṅkaḷ pala appar, campantarāl kuṟikkap
   paṭātavai. eṉavē, avaṟṟuḷ pala- tāḻaiyūr, takkaḷūr. taṇṭaṉ tōṭṭam,
   teṉṉūr, tañcākkai, teḷḷāṟu mutaliyaṉa appar campantarkkuppiṉṉum ivarkku
   muṉṉum ēṟpaṭṭaṉavātal vēṇṭum. ivar maṉaiviyar iruvaraip peṟṟavar. ivar
   varalāṟu muḻutum ivar patikaṅkaḷil aṟiyappaṭukiṉṟatu. ivar iṟaivaṉaic
   cakamārkkattil (naṇpar muṟaiyil) vaḻipaṭṭa aṭiyārāvar. ivar civaṉai
   nōkki añcātu kūṟum coṟkaḷ ivaratu paktiyiṉ aḻuttattai naṉku
   viḷakkukiṉṟaṉa.
   "paravaikkum eṉakkum paṟṟāya perumāṉē"
       "paravaikkum caṅkilikkum eṉakkum paṟṟāya perumāṉē"

   eṉṟu ivar pāṭiyuḷḷa toṭarkaḷ ivaratu uṇmaikkātal vāḻviṉaiyum civaneṟip
   paṟṟaiyum cevvitiṉ viḷakkuvatākum. ivaruṭaiya pāṭalkaḷ pala, centamiṟ
   iṉpam tōyntavai. iyaṟkai varuṇaṉai niṟainta kīḻvarum varikaḷaikkāṇka:
   1 "maṇṭapamuṅ kōpuramum māḷikai cūḷikaiyum
       maṟaiyoliyum viḻavoliyum maṟukuniṟai veytik
       kaṇṭavarkaṇ maṇaṅkavarum puṇṭarikap poykaik
       kārikaiyar kuṭaittāṭuṅ kalayanallūr kāṇē"
       2 "teṟṟukoṭi mullaiyoṭu mallikaiceṇ pakamun
       tiraiporutu varupuṉalcēr ariciliṉ teṉ karaimēl
       kaṟṟiṉam*naṉ karumpiṉmuḷai kaṟikaṟkak kaṟavai
       kamaḻkaḻunīr kavarkaḻaṉik kalayanallūr kāṇē"

   immūvar aṟivurai
   immūvarum caivacamayap potumakkaḷukkuk kūṟiya aṟivuraikaḷ yāvai?
   "camaṇa-pautta nūlkaḷ poynnūlkaḷ; avaṟṟai nampāmal civaṉai
   vaḻipaṭuṅkaḷ; tīviṉai aṟṟu naṉmai aṭaivīrkaḷ; tiruppatikaṅkaḷaip pāṭip
   payaṉ peṟuṅkaḷ: evarum ainteḻuttōti mēṉmai peṟalām; ainteḻuttē nalla
   tuṇai; aimpoṟikaḷaiyum aṭakkuṅkaḷ; maṉattai oruvaḻippaṭutti iṟaivaṉai
   niṉaiyuṅkaḷ; civaṉaik kāṇpīrkaḷ; pulaṉaṭakkam koṇṭu maṉattai
   oruvaḻippaṭutta vallavar civappēṟu peṟuvar" eṉpaṉa ivartam
   aṟivuraikaḷākum. campantaratu tirukkōṭikā tiruppatiyamum, neñcukku
   aṟivuṟuttum muṟaiyil pāṭappaṭṭa apparatu ārūrttiruttāṇṭakamum,
   makkaḷukku ēṟṟa camaya aṟivuraikaḷākak koḷaḷaṟpālaṉa. pala patikaṅkaḷil
   kōvil vaḻipāṭu, kōvil toṇṭu ceytal, tala yāttirai, aṭiyār kūṭṭuṟavu,
   aṭiyārkku uṇavaḷittal eṉpaṉavum vaṟpuṟuttappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
   eṭṭām tirumuṟai
   maṇivācakar pāṭiya tiruvācakamum tirukkōvaiyārum eṭṭām tirumuṟai
   eṉappaṭum; ivar kālattup pāṇṭiya maṉṉaṉ ciṟanta civapaktaṉ. avaṉ
   iraṇṭām varakuṇaṉ eṉpatu aṟiñar palar karuttu. maṇivācakar kālattil,
   caṅkararatu atvaitak koḷkai nāṭṭil mikutiyākap piracāram ceyyappaṭṭatu
   pōlum! ivar ataṉai "miṇṭiya māyāvātam eṉṉum caṇṭamāṟutam cuḻittaṭittu
   ārkkiṟatu." eṉṟu kuṟittuḷḷār. peṇkaḷ palavakai viḷaiyāṭṭukkaḷiṉpōtu
   iṟaivaṉuṭaiya ciṟappiyalpukaḷaip palapaṭiyāka eṭuttuk kūṟi āṭuvatāka
   ivar pāṭiyuḷḷār. avviḷaiyāṭṭukkaḷ (1) ammāṉai, (2) poṟcuṇṇam iṭittal
   (3) tumpiyāṭal (4) teḷḷēṇam koṭṭal (5) cāḻal (6) tiruppūvalli koytal,
   (7) untipaṟattal (8) tōḷ nōkkam āṭal (9) ūcalāṭal eṉpaṉa. ivvakai
   viḷaiyāṭṭukkaḷil iṟaivaṉ pukaḻaip pāṭiyāṭutal campantar kālattilum
   iruntatu eṉpatu campantar pākkaḷāl terikiṟatu.
   tiruvācakam
   mutal ēḻu tirumuṟaikaḷ paṇṇōṭum tāḷattōṭum pāṭattakkavai. āyiṉ,
   tiruvācakap pāṭalkaḷuḷ perumpālaṉa attakaiyavai alla; muṉṉavaṟṟuḷ
   camayap piracāram kāṇappaṭum; tiruvācakattil atu kāṇappeṟātu;
   māṇikkavācakar eṅṅaṉam uḻaṉṟu uḻaṉṟu iṟaivaṉatu aruḷaip peṟṟār eṉpatē
   innūliṟ kāṇappaṭuvatu. itu paṭippār uḷḷattai urukkum taṉmai vāyntatu.
   "uṉ kuṟaikaḷai oppuk koṇṭu kaṭavuḷukkumuṉ aḻu: avaṉai aṭaiyalām" eṉpatē
   tiruvācakattiṉ uyirnāṭip pakutikaḷil oṉṟu. iṅṅaṉam tam kuṟaikaḷai
   uḷḷavāṟu uṇarntu varuntik kaṭavuḷ muṉ aḻutal eṉpatu ellōrkkum eḷitil
   iyalvatu aṉṟu; "kaṭavuḷiṭam eṉṟum iṟavāta kātal peṟa virumpum paktaṉ,
   aṉpai aṭip paṭaiyākak koṇṭa meyyaṭiyāruṭaṉ toṭarntu paḻakavēṇṭum"
   eṉpatu caiva cittāntak koḷkaikaḷuḷ oṉṟu. itaṉaic civañāṉa pōtam 12-ām
   cūttiram naṉku vaṟpuṟuttukiṟatu. māṇikkavācakar ittakaiya aṭiyār
   kuḻāttil tammai vaikkumāṟu iṟaivaṉai vēṇṭukiṟār. ivviraṇṭum
   tiruvācakattiṉ uyarnāṭi eṉṟu kūṟalām.
   tirukkōvaiyār
   āṉmāvākiya talaivaṉ kaṭavuḷākiya talaiviyaip pala cōtaṉaip paṭikaḷaik
   kaṭantu kūṭutalē tirukkōvaiyār eṉṉum nūliṟ kuṟikkappaṭum poruḷākum.
   pākkaḷ iṉimaiyum poruḷāḻamum uṭaiyavai.
   oṉpatām tirumuṟai
   itaṉil tirumāḷikait tēvar mutaliya oṉpatu aṭiyārkaḷ pāṭiya pākkaḷ
   irukkiṉṟaṉa. ovvoru pāvum "tiru icaippā" eṉappaṭum. tiru māḷikait tēvar
   tiruviṭaimarutūr māḷikai maṭattaic cērntavar. ivar tillaiyaip paṟṟiya
   nāṉku icaippākkaḷ pāṭiyuḷḷār.
   cēntaṉār tiruvīḻimiḻalai, tiruvāvaṭutuṟai, tiruviṭaikkaḻi
   ākiyavaṟṟiṉmītu mūṉṟu tiruvicaip pākkaḷum, tillaimītu tiruppallāṇṭum
   pāṭiyuḷḷār.
   karuvūrttēvar mutalām irācarācaṉ kālattavar. ivar camāti periyakōvil
   tiruccuṟṟil irukkiṟatu. ipperiyār, tillai, tirukkaḷantai- ātittēcvaram,
   kīḻkkōṭṭūr-maṇiyampalam, tiru mukattalai, tirailōkkiya cuntaram, kaṅkai
   koṇṭa cōḻēccaram, tiruppūvaṇam, tiruccāṭṭiyakkuṭi,
   tañcai-irācarācēccaram, tiruviṭai marutūr ākiya pattut talaṅkaḷmītu
   pattut tiru vicaippākkaḷ pāṭiyuḷḷār.
   pūnturutti nampi kāṭa nampi eṉpavar tiruppūn turuttiyaic cērntavar;
   ivar tiruvārūr, tillai eṉṉum irupakutikaḷmītu iraṇṭu tiruvicaip pākkaḷ
   pāṭiyuḷḷār. ipperiyār kaṇampullar, kaṇṇappar, tirunāvukkaracar,
   campantar, cuntarar, cēramāṉ perumāḷ ākiya nāyaṉmārait tam pākkaḷil
   pārāṭṭiyuḷḷār.
   kaṇṭarātittar, mutaṟ parāntaka cōḻaṉ makaṉār. ivar tillaiyaippaṟṟi oru
   tiruvicaippā pāṭiyuḷḷār. ataṉil tamtantai ciṟṟampalattaip poṉvēyntāṉ
   eṉpataik kuṟittuḷḷār.
   vēṇāṭṭu aṭikaḷ eṉpavar teṉtiruvāṅkūr pakutiyaic cērntavar. ivarum
   tillaiyaip paṟṟiyē oru tiruvicaippā pāṭiyuḷḷār.
   tiruvāli amutaṉār cīkāḻiyai aṭuttuḷḷa tiruvāli eṉṉum ūraic cērntavar.
   ivarum tillaiyaip paṟṟiyē nāṉku tiruvicaip pākkaḷ pāṭiyuḷḷār. "teṉṉaṉ
   tamiḻum icaiyum kalanta ciṟṟampalam" eṉṟu tam pāvil kuṟittatiliruntu,
   ivaruṭaiya tamiḻc cuvaiyum icaiyaṟivum pulaṉākiṉṟaṉa.
   puruṣōttama nampi eṉpavar tillayaip paṟ ṟiyē iraṇṭu tiruvicaip pākkaḷ
   pāṭiyuḷḷār. cētirāyar eṉpavar tirukkōvalūr aracar eṉṟu karututal takum.
   ivar tillaiyaippaṟṟi oru tiruvicaippā pāṭiyuḷḷār.
   tillaiyiṉ ciṟappu
   ivvoṉpatām tirumuṟaiyil pala pākkaḷ tillaiyaippaṟṟiyē kāṇpataṟkuriya
   kāraṇam yātu? aintoḻil iyaṟṟutaṟku ētuvākiya tirunaṭaṉam ceyyappaṭum
   oḷi nilaiyamākat tillai viḷaṅkuvatāl, ataṉai ellāt talaṅkaḷiṉum
   uyarntatākac caiva nūlkaḷ kūṟum. attuṭaṉ, mutaṟparāntakaṉ kālamutal
   cōḻavēntar tillaiyaip pallaṟṟāṉum ciṟappittu vantatum, tillaip
   perumāṉait tam kulanāyakaṉ eṉṟu kūṟivantatum, tillaiyaic cōḻar kālattil
   uyarttiviṭṭaṉa. ittakaiya kāraṇaṅkaḷāl tāṉ tillai mēṟcoṉṉa
   āciriyarkaḷāl peritum pōṟṟappaṭṭatu eṉṉalām.
   pattām tirumuṟai
   tirumūlar pāṭiya tirumantiram pattām tirumuṟai. itupaṟṟiya vivaraṅkaḷ
   ceṉṟa kaṭṭuraiyiṟ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
   patiṉōrām tirumuṟai
   itaṉuṭ kūṟappaṭṭa pākkaḷiṉ-nūlkaḷiṉ āciriyar (1) civapirāṉ, (2)
   kāraikkālammaiyār, (3) aiyaṭikaḷ kāṭavarkōṉ, (4) cēramāṉ perumāḷ
   nāyaṉār, (5) nakkīratēvanāyaṉār, (6) kallāṭa tēva nāyaṉār, (7)
   kapilatēva nāyaṉār, (8) paraṇa tēva nāyaṉār, (9) iḷamperumāṉ aṭikaḷ,
   (10) atirā aṭikaḷ, (11) paṭṭiṉattup piḷḷaiyār, (12) nampiyāṇṭār nampi
   eṉpōrāvar.
   (1) civapirāṉ pāṇapattirarukkup poṉ tarumpaṭi cēramāṉ perumāḷukku
   viṭutta oru pāṭal ittirumuṟaiyil mutalil kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.
   (2) kāraikkālammaiyār tiruvālaṅkāṭṭu mūtta tiruppatikam, tiru
   iraṭṭaimaṇimālai, aṟputat tiruvantāti eṉṉum mūṉṟu nūlkaḷaip pāṭiyuḷḷār.
   (3) aiyaṭikaḷ kāṭavarkōṉ eṉṟa pallavar ovvoru civattalattaiyum paṟṟip
   pāṭiya kṣēttira veṇpā eṉṉum nūl aṭuttu iṭam peṟṟuḷḷatu.
   4. cēramāṉ perumāḷ-poṉ vaṇṇattantāti, tiru ārūr mummaṇikkōvai,
   tirukkailāya ñāṉaulā eṉṉum mūṉṟaiyum pāṭiyuḷḷār.
   5. nakkīratēvanāyaṉār-kaṇṇappa tēvar tirumaṟam mutaliya pala nūlkaḷaip
   pāṭiyuḷḷār.
   6. kallāṭatēvanāyaṉār-ivarum kaṇṇapparaip paṟṟi muppatteṭṭu varikaḷ
   koṇṭa akavaṟpā pāṭiyuḷḷār.
   7. kapilatēva nāyaṉār – ivar vināyakarmēl iraṭṭai maṇimālai pāṭiyuḷḷār.
   8. paraṇatēva nāyaṉār civaperumāṉ tiruvantāti eṉṟa nūlaip pāṭiyuḷḷār.
   9. iḷamperumāṉaṭikaḷ eṉpavar oru patikam pāṭiyuḷḷār.
   10. atikarā aṭikaḷ vināyakarmītu mummaṇikkōvai pāṭiyavar.
   11. paṭṭiṉattaṭikaḷ kōvil nāṉmaṇimālai, tirukkaḻumala mummaṇikkōvai,
   tiruviṭai marutūr mummaṇikkōvai, tiruvēkampa muṭaiyār tiruvantāti,
   tiruvoṟṟiyūr orupā orupaḵtu eṉṉum nūlkaḷaip pāṭiyuḷḷār. ivai paṭikkap
   paṭikka iṉpam taruvaṉa. kōyil nāṉmaṇi mālaiyil,
   "...niṉ
       tamarpeyar eḻutiya varineṭum puttakat
       teṉṉaiyum eḻuta vēṇṭuvaṉ"

   eṉavarum aṭikaḷ uḷḷattai īrkkattakkavai.
   12. nampiyāṇṭār nampi pattuc ciṟunūlkaḷaip pāṭiyuḷḷār. avaṟṟuḷ āṟu
   nūlkaḷ campantaraip paṟṟiyavai. avai (1) campantar tiruvantāti (2)
   campantar -tiruccaṇpai- viruttam. (3) campantar-tirumummaṇikkōvai, (4)
   campantar tiruvulāmālai, (5) campantar tirukkalampakam (6) campantar
   tiruttokai eṉpaṉa. inta āṟilum campantar varalāṟṟuk kuṟippukkaḷ
   aṉaittum aṭaṅki viṭṭaṉa. (7) tirunāvukkaracar- tiru ēkātaca mālaiyil
   apparaip paṟṟiya ciṟanta varalāṟṟuk kuṟippukkaḷ kāṇkiṉṟaṉa. (8) kōyil
   tiruppaṇṇiyar viruttattil kaṇṇappar, cēramāṉ, cuntarar, cākkiyar,
   atipattar kalikkāmar, māṇikkavācakar, varakuṇapāṇṭiyaṉ ākiyōr
   kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷar. (9) vināyakar tiru iraṭṭai maṇimālai 20 ceyyuṭkaḷaik
   koṇṭatu. (10) tiruttoṇṭar-tiru antāti eṉpatu cuntarar pāṭiya
   tiruttoṇṭat tokaiyaip piṉpaṟṟi viritturaikkappaṭṭa nūlākum. itil 63
   nāyaṉmār varalāṟṟuk kuṟippukkaḷum oṉpatu tokai aṭiyār paṟṟiya
   kuṟippukkaḷum kāṇkiṉṟaṉa. inta antāti cēkkiḻāratu periya purāṇattiṟkuc
   ciṟanta aṭippaṭaiyākum.
   paṉṉiraṇṭām tirumuṟai.
   itu cuntarar varalāṟṟaiyum avarāl tiruttoṇṭat tokaiyil kuṟikkappeṟṟa
   aṭiyār varalāṟukaḷaiyum kūṟum peruṅkāviyamākum. iḵtu ēṟattāḻa 4250
   ceyyuṭkaḷai uṭaiyatu. tamiḻnāṭṭil caiva camayam paṟṟic ceyyappaṭṭa
   mutaṟkāviyam ituvēyākum. itu tirumuṟaikaḷaiyum kaṉṉaparamparaic
   ceytikaḷaiyum, nāyaṉmār varalāṟukaḷai aṅkaṅkē kuṟikkum tillai- ulāp
   pōṉṟa piṟanūlkaḷaiyum, camaṇa-pautta camaya nūlkaḷaiyum, nāyaṉmār
   paṟṟiya kalveṭṭu- ciṟpa-ōviyac cāṉṟukaḷaiyum uṟukaruvikaḷākak koṇṭu
   ceyyappeṟṟatu. itu moḻipeyarppu nūlaṉṟu. nūlāciriyar kālattil naṭanta
   varalāṟum aṉṟu; avarkku muṉṉar ēṟattāḻak ki.pi.300 mutal 900 varai
   irunta nāyaṉmārkaḷaip paṟṟiya varalāṟu. nāyaṉmārō, vaṭakkē
   kāmpīliyiliruntu teṟkē maturai varaip pala nāṭukaḷil vāḻntavar-pala
   kālaṅkaḷil parantupaṭṭu vāḻntavar. pala cātiyār āvar.
   apperumakkaḷuṭaiya piṟappiṭam, cāti, ceyta camayattoṇṭu mutaliya
   ceytikaḷait tirumuṟaikaḷai maṭṭum nampi eṭukkāmal, tāmum naṉku
   vicārittu, pala iṭaṅkaṭkum nēril ceṉṟu ārāyntu, yāvaṟṟaiyum
   tiraṭṭikkoṇṭa piṟakē nūl pāṭiṉār eṉak karuta avar nūliṟ pala cāṉṟukaḷ
   uḷa. appar purāṇattil kuṇapara īcuvarattaiyum, ciṟuttoṇṭar purāṇattil
   vātāpip paṭaiyeṭuppaiyum avar kūṟiyirāviṭil, iṉṟu appar-campantar
   kālattai aṟiya vēṟu vaḻi illai. cēkkiḻār ciṟanta caivar; perumpulavar;
   ivaṟṟuṭaṉ cōḻap perunāṭṭiṉ mutalamaiccar ātaliṉ, avaratu kāviyattil
   ilakkiya nayam- varalāṟṟu uṇmaikaḷ-caiva cittānta karuttukkaḷ eṉpaṉa
   aṅkaṅkē miḷirkiṉṟaṉa.
   caṅka kālattiṟkup piṟaku tamiḻarāya cōḻarāṭciyil tamiḻnāṭṭu nāyaṉmāraip
   paṟṟit tamiḻar pāṭiya tamiḻk kāviyam iḵtu oṉṟē eṉpatu
   kavaṉikkattakkatu. itaṉkaṇ pirāmaṇar mutal paṟaiyar īṟāka uḷḷa palacāti
   makkaḷiṉ paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷ, pēccu muṟaikaḷ, nāṭṭiṉ perum
   pirivukaḷ-uṭpirivukaḷ, nāṭṭu āṭcimuṟai, camaṇa-pautta-caiva camayak
   karuttukkaḷ iṉṉa piṟavum viḷakkamākat tarappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. itaṉkaṇ
   kiṭaikkum ic ceytikaḷ cōḻarkāla ilakkiyaṅkaḷ etaṉilum kiṭaikkā. oru
   pakkam pallavar kāla varalāṟṟaiyum maṟṟoru pakkam cōḻarkāla nāṭṭu
   nilaiyaiyum, moḻi nilaiyaiyum, caiva camaya nilaiyaiyum, uḷḷaṭakkik
   koṇṭuḷḷa innūl, tamiḻar naṟpēṟṟiṉ payaṉāyt tōṉṟiyatu eṉṉal mikaiyākātu.
   in nūliliruntu cēkkiḻāratu ilakkiyap pulamai, ilakkaṇappulamai,
   tirumuṟaip pulamai, icai- naṭaṉam-maruttuvam-vāṉanūl mutaliya palkalaip
   pulamai, caiva cittānta aṟivu mutaliyavaṟṟai naṉkaṟiyalām. ivai
   virippiṟ perukum. periyapurāṇam ēṟpaṭṭa piṉṉarē aṟupattumūṉṟu
   nāyaṉmārkaṭkum ciṟpaṅkaḷ ēṟpaṭṭaṉa; viḻākkaḷ perukiṉa eṉṉum
   vivaraṅkaḷaic cōḻarkālak kōvilkaḷiliruntum kalveṭṭukkaḷiliruntum
   naṉkaṟiyalām.
   -------------------------

                        9. cittānta cāttiraṅkaḷ

   caivacittānta cāttiraṅkaḷ
   1. tiruvuntiyār, 2. tirukkaḷiṟṟuppaṭiyār, 3. civañāṉa pōtam, 4.
   civañāṉa cittiyār, 5. irupā-irupaḵtu, 6. uṇmai viḷakkam,
   7.civappirakācam, 8. tiruvaruṭ payaṉ, 9. viṉā-veṇpā, 10. pōṟṟip
   paḵṟoṭai, 11. koṭikkavi, 12. neñcuviṭutūtu, 13. uṇmai neṟi viḷakkam,
   14. caṅkaṟpa nirākaraṇam eṉpaṉa caiva cittānta cāttiraṅkaḷ,. ivai
   ovvoṉṟaip paṟṟiyum, iṭac curukkam karuti, ōraḷavu iṅkuk kāṇalām.
   1. tiruvuntiyār.
   itu civañāṉa pōtattiṟku muṟpaṭṭa nūl. itaṉaic ceytavar tiruviyalūr
   uyyavanta tēvanāyaṉār [1]. innūl civañāṉa pōtattaip pōlac cittāntak
   karuttukkaḷaik kōvaipaṭak kūṟātu, avvappōtu āciriyarkkut tōṉṟiya
   uṇarcci anupavattaik kūṟum 45 ceyyuṭkaḷai uṭaiyatu. āyiṉ, ivvanupavap
   pāṭalkaḷ, camayacāttiraṅkaḷ pōlavē, kaṭavuḷ-uyir-ulakam eṉṉum mūṉṟu
   poruḷkaḷiṉ ilakkaṇattaiyum, payaṉkaḷaiyum, payaṉ aṭaiyum neṟikaḷaiyum
   viḷakkuvaṉavākum.
   ------------
   [1]. innūl ki.pi. 1147-il ceyyappaṭṭateṉpar- cittānta cāttiram, iraṇṭām
       patippu; P.669.

   kaṭavuḷ nilai
   paramporuḷ collaiyum maṉattaiyum kaṭantu niṟpatu; iyaṟkaiyākavē malam
   aṟṟatu; aṟitaṟku aṟiyatu; āyiṉum uyirkaḷ uyyumāṟu karuṇaiyāka
   viḷaṅkuvatu; avvaruḷ kāraṇamāka aruvattiliruntu uruvamāka
   veḷippaṭuvatu; atu tāṉākavē avvuruvaikkoḷḷum. apparamporuḷ, paṭaittal,
   kāttal, aḻittal, maṟaittal, aruḷal eṉṉum aintoḻilkaḷaiyum uyirkaṭkuk
   kūṭṭuvatu; pāvaṉaikku appāṟpaṭṭatu; āṉma aṟivāl aṟiyappaṭātatu;
   uyirkaḷaip pakkuvap paṭuttuvataṟkākak kaḷḷaṉaippōla maṟaintu niṉṟu,
   tiyāṉam-mantiram-caiva vēṭam ivaṟṟāl uyirkaḷai nalvaḻippaṭac ceyvatu;
   pakkuvam mutirntapōtu kuruvaṭivil veḷippaṭṭu, tīṭcai ceytu, uṇmai
   ñāṉattai uṇarttuvatu; uyirkaḷ ceytavattiṟkup payaṉ aḷippatu; taṉ taṉmai
   aruḷi, uyirkaḷai vīṭu peṟacceyvatu.[2]
   -----------
   [2]. ce. 1,5,9,12,13,20, 22.

   uyiriṉ iyalpu
   uyirkaḷ pala. avai pāca pantattāl kaṭṭuppaṭṭavai. mutti nilaiyil pācam
   eṉṉum malam aṟṟu niṟpavai. meññāṉa niṣṭai kūṭiya makkaḷ piṟavip
   peruṅkaṭal nīntiṉavarāvar; avarkaḷ ñāṉakkaṇṇāl nōkkum iyalpiṉar;
   kacinta uḷḷam uṭaiyavar ātaliṉ kaṭavuḷai uṇarum cakti peṟṟavarāvar,.
   mutti nilaiyiṟ civamān taṉmai peṟṟavarāyiṉum uyirkaḷ uyirkaḷē. [3]
   civamāntaṉmai peṟa vaḻi
   makkaḷ malam nīṅki niṟṟaṟku muyaṟci, civaṉpāl maṟavāta aṉpuṭaimai,
   civattaip paṟṟiyē eṇṇiyiruttal eṉpavai vaḻikaḷ ākum; ivaṟṟāl ulakiṉpāṟ
   cellum paṟṟuc civattiṉpāl cellum; vaḻipāṭu uṟutippaṭum; annilaiyil
   civam kuruvāka vantu, civa-tīṭcai ceytu, civañāṉam cērkkum. ataṉāl
   tiruvaruṭcakti maṉattiṉkaṇ poruntiyirukkum.[4]
   mukti nilai
   itu colloṇā iyalpuṭaiyatu. aṟiviṟku eṭṭāta uyarnilaiyatu; tiruvaruḷ
   peṟṟavar iṉpuṟu nilai. innilai aṭaintōrē, "toṇṭar" eṉappaṭuvar. naṉavil
   tūya mēl nilaiyil iruppavar. avarkaṭkuc ciṟṟiṉpamum pēriṉpam ākum.
   avarkaḷ pittaraip pōlak kāṇappaṭuvar. āyiṉ, avarkaḷ karuttum uyirum
   iṟaivaṉ niṉaivai viṭṭu nīṅkā iyalpiṉa. [5]
   ----------
   [3]. ce. 2,3,7,11,36.
       [4]. ce, 8, 10, 14, 16, 18, 19, 21, 23, 25, 27, 28, 34, 37-40.
       [5]. ce. 4,6,29,30,31, 33-35.

   2. tirukkaḷiṟṟuppaṭiyār
   ituvum civañāṉa pōtattiṟku muṟpaṭṭa nūl. itaṉaic ceytavar mēṟcoṉṉa
   uyyavanta tēva nāyaṉār māṇavarkku māṇavar. ivar tirukkaṭavūr uyyavanta
   tēva nāyaṉār eṉṉum peyar koṇṭavar. [6] innūl 100 veṇpākkaḷai uṭaiyatu.
   tiruvuntiyāriṉ karuttaic cevvaiyāka viḷakkuvatu. itu poṉṉampalattiluḷḷa
   tirukkaḷiṟṟuppaṭiyil vaikkappaṭṭup pārāṭṭap paṭṭatātaliṉ, tirukkaḷiṟṟup
   paṭiyār eṉak kāraṇap peyar peṟṟatu.[7] innūlil, nāyaṉmār
   varalāṟukaḷiliruntum utāraṇaṅkaḷ tantu valviṉai, melviṉai eṉpaṉa
   viḷakkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. cāttira viḷakkaṅkaḷ cila uvamaikaḷāl
   terivikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. tirukkuṟaṭpākkaḷ cilavaṟṟai mēṟkōḷākak koṇṭu
   avaṟṟiṉ poruḷai viḷakkum ceyyuṭkaḷ iṉpam payappaṉa. [8]
   -----------
   [6]. ivar kālam ki.pi. 1177 eṉpar-cittānta cāttiram, iraṇṭām patippu P
       669
       [7] .S.C.P. 33.
       [8]. "cārpuṇarntu cārpu keṭavoḻuki ṉeṉṟamaiyāṟ cārpuṇartal tāṉē
       tiyāṉamumām-cārpu keṭavoḻuki ṉalla cāmatiyumāṅ kētap paṭavaruva
       tillaiviṉaip paṟṟu." (ce. 34)

   iṟai
   paramporuḷ cakti-civamāka viḷaṅkum. avvāṟu viḷaṅkuvatu ulakiṟkut tāyum
   tantaiyumākiya nilaiyākum. [9] tūya poṉṉiṉ niṟam, nakaikaḷāka māṟiya
   pōtilum poṟkaṭṭiyāy niṉṟapōtum oru taṉmaiyāy viḷaṅkutal pōla, iṟaivaṉ
   ulakil attuvitamākak kalantu oṉṟaṉṟāy iraṇṭaṉṟāy niṟpaṉ.[10] kaṭal
   nīriliruntu uppu viḷaivatupōla, iṟaivaṉ aruḷālē tirumēṉi koḷvāṉ. ivvāṟu
   koḷḷum tirumēṉikaḷuḷ āciriyat tirumēṉi ciṟappuṭaiyatu.
   ------------
   [9] ce. 1
       "vēṇṭuṅkāl vēṇṭum piṟavāmai yeṉṟamaiyāl
       vēṇṭiṉaḵ toṉṟumē vēṇṭuvatu-vēṇṭiṉatu
       vēṇṭāmai vēṇṭavaru meṉṟamaiyāl vēṇṭiṭuka
       vēṇṭāmai vēṇṭumavaṉ pāl." (ce.45.)

   kaṭal nīrai mukantu ceṉṟu naṉṉīr poḻiyum mukilpōla -āciriyaṉāka
   veḷippaṭum iṟaivaṉ anupavap payaṉai aṟivuraiyāka upatēcippaṉ.[11]
   iṟaivaṉ meyñāṉikaḷaic cārntu āvēcittu niṉṟu upatēcittalum uṇṭu.
   ivvupatēcam meyññāṉikaḷatu ceyalaṉṟu; iṟaivaṉatu ceyalē ākum.[12].
   malam ellām nāṟṟam uṭaiyaṉavāyiṉum, tūymai poruntiya pacuviṉ cāṇam
   piṟamalaṅkaḷai ellām nīkkutalpōla āciriyaṉ makkaḷuḷ oruvaṉē eṉiṉum,
   civaṉ avaṉai nilaikkaḷamākak kōṭalāl, avaṉ piṟaviyai oḻikka
   vallavaṉāvaṉ. [13] kaṭalakattē alaikaḷāl alaippuṟātu āṭumpaṭi
   niṉṟavarkkum kaṭaliṉ āḻattaiyum akalattaiyum aḷattal aritu. atupōlak
   kaṭavuḷ aruḷ peṟṟavarāyiṉum avarkaḷ avaṉatu muḻup perumaiyai
   aṟintavarākār. [14] uyirkaḷ, tam aṟiviṟ civam viḷaṅkat talaivaṉaiyum
   (kaṭavuḷaiyum) tammaiyum uṇarum; uṇarnta piṟaku uṭaluṭaṉ iruppiṉum
   paṟṟu nīṅkappeṟṟuc cettāraip pōlat tiriyum; avai civaṉ vayappaṭṭu
   niṟkumātalāl mīṇṭum ulaka nikaḻccikaḷil pukā.[15] āṟṟunīr kaṭalil
   kalantu kaṭal nīrāki, mīṇṭum āṟṟiṉ paṭukaiyil mēlēṟiṉum upputtaṉmai
   māṟuvatillai. atupōla, civattōṭu oṉṟi niṟkum māyññāṉikaḷiṉatu aṟi-
   ceyaṟkaruvikaḷ māyai nilaiyil illāmal civa karaṇamāka viḷaṅkum. [16]
   avarkaḷatu paṟṟaṟṟa nilai maṇiyiṉkaṇ tūṅkum nākkiṟku oppākum. [17]
   paṭikam taṉṉaic cārntuḷḷa poruḷiṉatu niṟattaik kāṭṭum. taṉatu niṟattaik
   kāṭṭātu. atupōla ulakattaic cārntu avvaṇṇamāy iruttalum, mutti nilaiyil
   iṟaivaṉait taḻuviniṉṟu avaṉ vaṇṇamāka iruttalum uyir nilaiyākum [18].
   -----------
   [10]. ce.79. [11]. ce.90. [12]. ce.66 [13]. ce.7.
       [14]. ce.90. [15]. ce.47 [16]. ce.11. [17]. ce.32 [18]. ce.97

   cātaṉam
   cariyai, kiriyai, yōkam, ñāṉam, eṉpaṉa uyirkaṭkuc cātaṉa neṟikaḷākum.
   civaṉaṟam- valviṉai, melviṉai eṉa iruvakaippaṭum. iṟaivaṉait tuṇaiyeṉak
   karuti oḻukuvatum, pūcai, tiyāṉam, pāṭal, āṭal mutaliyaṉa nikaḻttuvatum
   melviṉai eṉappaṭum. [19] ciṟuttoṇṭar piḷḷai arintatum, caṇṭīcar
   tantaiyaik koṉṟatum, arivāḷ tāyar tam kaḻuttaiyē ariya muyaṉṟatum
   valviṉaic civaṉaṟamākum. [20] tiruvuruva vaḻipāṭum, āṟu ātāraṅkaḷil
   iṟaivaṉait tiyāṉittalum, "ātātara yōkam" eṉappaṭum. iṟaivaṉai akaṇṭamāy
   aruḷ veḷiyil mauṉamāka iruntu vaḻipaṭṭu avaṉōṭu oṟṟumaiyuṟṟu niṟṟal
   "nirātāra yōkam" eṉappaṭum [21]. ainteḻuttu (pañcāṭcaram) iṟaivaṉaik
   kāṭṭum iyalpuṭaiyatu. nāta tattuvattiṟku appāl uḷḷa iṟaivaṉai, yōka
   muṭivil kēṭkum cilampoli vaḻiyē ceṉṟu aṭaiya muyalavēṇṭum. [22] "nāṉ
   piramam" eṉpatu mayakkavuṇarcciyēyākum. [23] uyiriṉiṭam civa
   vaṭivamākiya ñāṉattaik kaṇṭu vaṇaṅkic civaperumāṉukku aṉpu nīrāl
   tirumañcaṉamāṭṭit taṉṉai avaṉukku vāṭāta oru malarākac cārtti vaḻipaṭiṉ
   piṟavi oḻiyum. [24] aṟivu neṟiyum aṉpu neṟiyum piṟappai aṟukka
   vallavai.[25]
   ------------
   [19]. ce. 16-18. [20]. ce. 18-20 [21]. ce. 22.
       [22]. ce. 25,33. [23].ce. 85 [24]. ce.44 [25].ce.100

   iṟaivaṉai aṇaintōr ilakkaṇam
   iṟaivaṉai aṇaintavarē aṭiyārāvar. ālamarattait taṉṉakattē aṭakkiya
   vittup pōlat tiruvaruḷait taṉṉakattē aṭakkiyavar aṭiyār. [26] iṟaivaṉ
   perumaiyai aṭiyār aṟivar. aṭiyār perumaiyai iṟaivaṉ aṟivāṉ. itaṟkuk
   kaṇṇapparum kāḷattiyapparumē cāṉṟāvar.[27] iṟaivaṉ pakti
   valaiyiṟpaṭupavaṉ eṉpatu teḷiyalām. [28] ñāṉa neṟi niṉṟavarkkuk kālam,
   iṭam, ticai, irukkai eṉpaṉa illai [29]. civañāṉamum, civapōkamum vittum
   muḷaiyum pōlvaṉa.[30]
   -----------
   [26].ce. 57. [27].ce.52.
       [28].ce. 52. "cēntaṉāramutu" eṉṟu ippoḻutum "tiruvātirai-kaḷi" eṉṟu
       tillaip perumāṉukku nivētippatu vaḻakkam.
       [29].ce.27-30 [30].ce. 57.

   3. civañāṉapōtam
   itu mēṟcoṉṉa 14 cāttiraṅkaḷil talaiciṟantatu. cittānta uṇmaikaḷ
   kōvaipaṭac ceyyappaṭṭa centamiḻ nūl. tiruveṇṇey nallūr-meykaṇṭa tēvar
   eṉṉum vēḷāḷa aṟiñar ceytatu; urainaṭaip pakutiyum ceyyuṭ pakutiyum
   uṭaiyatu; urainaṭaippakuti mēṟ kōḷkaḷaiyum ētukkaḷaiyum uṭaiyatu;
   ceyyuṭ pakuti 12 cūttiraṅkaḷākavum 81 veṇpākkaḷākavum uḷḷatu.
   ippaṉṉiraṇṭu cūttiraṅkaḷum raurava ākamattil uḷḷa 12 vaṭamoḻic
   cūttiraṅkaḷiṉ moḻi peyarppu eṉpatu ṭākṭar ramaṇa cāstiri pōṉṟa orucār
   aṟiñar karuttu.[31] cuvāmi vētācalam, kā. cuppiramaṇiya piḷḷai pōṉṟa
   aṟiñarkaḷ atu mutal nūlē eṉṟu karutukiṉṟaṉar. [32] teṉṉintiya caiva
   cittānta makā camājattiṉ talaivarāṉa M.pālacuppiramaṇiya mutaliyār
   (B.A., B.L.,) civañāṉa pōtam moḻipeyarppaṉṟu eṉpataṟku 120 kāraṇaṅkaḷ
   kāṭṭi 1949-il ciṟunūl oṉṟai veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷār. [33]
   meykaṇṭār ki.pi. 13-ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ muṟpakutiyil vāḻntavar. ivar parañcōti
   mā muṉivar eṉṉum periyāriṭam ñāṉōpatēcam peṟṟavar. [34] ivar nūlil uḷḷa
   mutal mūṉṟu cūttiraṅkaḷ piramāṇa-iyal eṉavum, aṭutta mūṉṟum
   ilakkaṇa-iyal eṉavum, aṭutta mūṉṟu cūttiraṅkaḷ cātaṉa-iyal eṉavum,
   kaṭaici mūṉṟum payaṉ-iyal eṉavum peyarpeṟum.
   ---------
   [31]. Colos, 2, part I, P.531 including foot-note No. 43.
       [32]. S.C. PP. 45-73; M.S. PP. 11-27.
       [33]. itaṉ vilai aṇā 2. 34. S.M.P.9.

   piramāṇa -iyal pati, pācam, pacu eṉṉum mūṉṟum uṇmai eṉpatai 3
   cūttiraṅkaḷāl viḷaṅkum. ilakkaṇa- iyal pacu, pācam, pati eṉṉum mūṉṟaṉ
   ilakkaṇaṅkaḷai muṟaiyē mūṉṟu cūttiraṅkaḷāl viḷakkum. cātaṉa-iyal eṉpatu
   cātaṉam aṭaitaṟkuriyavar uyirkaḷ eṉpataiyum, iṟaivaṉ tirumēṉi koṇṭu
   ñāṉam uṇarttalaiyum, aṅṅaṉam ñāṉam kaivarap peṟṟa uyir ainteḻuttu ōtip
   payaṉpeṟalum muṟaiyē mūṉṟu cūttiraṅkaḷil viḷakkum. payaṉ-iyal eṉpatu
   pāca nīkkam, civappēṟu, civappēṟṟiṉai nilai niṟutta nikaḻvaṉa iṉṉavai
   eṉpaṉa muṟaiyē mūṉṟu cūttiraṅkaḷil viḷakkum. ivvuṇmaikaḷ yāvum tarukka
   muṟaiyil nilai niṟuttappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
   1. piramāṇa iyal
   mutaṟ cūttirattil kaṭavuḷ uṇmaiyum, avar ellāvaṟṟaiyum oṭukki mīḷat
   tōṟṟuvikkum mutaṟ perumāṉākiya civaṉ eṉpatum kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. kaṭavuḷ
   uyirkaḷuṭaṉ attu vitamāka niṉṟu avaikaṭku viṉaippayaṉai ūṭṭum muṟaiyum
   uyirkaṭku malapantattāl piṟappu iṟappu nikaḻum muṟaiyum 2-ām
   cūttirattiṟ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. mūṉṟām cūttiram uyir cūṉyattai aṟivatu,
   paruvuṭal, aṟikaruvikaḷ, nuṇṇuṭal, pirāṇavāyu mutaliyavaṟṟiṟku
   vēṟāṉatu, tāṉē aṟivataṉṟi uṇartta uṇarvatu, uṭaṟ karaṇaṅkaḷ
   kūṭiyiruppiṉum avaṟṟiṟku vēṟāy niṉṟu aṟivatu eṉpaṉa kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
   2. ilakkaṇa iyal
   uṭkāraṇaṅkaḷ ākum cittam, maṉam, akaṅkāram, putti eṉpavavaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu
   aṉṟāy vēṟāy niṟpatu uyir. atu anāti kāla mutalē āṇava malattāṟ kaṭṭuṇṭu
   kiṭappatu. puṟa-akak karuvikaḷum uyirum amaiccum aracum pōla
   viḷaṅkuvaṉa. uyirkaṭku akkaruvikaḷ tuṇai ceyvaṉa. ivai yāvum
   toḻiṟpaṭuṅkālam uyirkaṭku "naṉavu nilai" eṉappaṭum. ivaṟṟuṭ cila
   kuṟainta kālam uyirkaṭkuk "kaṉavu nilai"; mēlum cila kuṟainta poḻutu
   "uṟakka nilai" eṉappaṭum. ivai yāvum nīṅki uyir taṉṉilai niṉṟapōtu
   "pēruṟakka nilai" eṉappaṭum. uyiriṉ akaṅkāramum oṭuṅkiya nilai "uyirppu
   aṭakkam" eṉappaṭum. ivai aṉaittum nāṉkām cūttirattuṭ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷa
   ceytikaḷ.
   karuvikaḷ aṟivaṟṟaṉa ātaliṉ tammaiyum aṟiyā; tammaic celuttum
   uyiriṉaiyum aṟiyā; avaipōla uyirkaḷum tammai aṟiyā; tammaic celuttum
   iṟaivaṉaiyum aṟiyā eṉpatu aintām cūttirattup poruḷākum. cattu, acattu
   eṉṉum iru moḻikaṭkum ilakkaṇam uraippatu āṟām cūttiram. "cattu" eṉṟum
   *keṭiṉṟi viḷaṅkum poruḷ. eṉavē, iṟai oṉṟē avvilakkaṇattiṟku
   ilakkiyamākum; āyiṉum caiva cittāntattil mupporuḷkaḷākiya iṟai, uyir,
   ulakam eṉpaṉa eṉṟum uḷḷa poruḷkaḷāka oppukkoḷḷappaṭṭavai. ātaliṉ avai
   mūṉṟum "cattē" ākum. uyirum ulakamum vikāramaṭaikiṉṟamaiyiṉ, acattātal
   peṟappaṭum. immūṉṟiṉaiyum vēṟu pirittu aṟitaṟku avai muṟaiyē
   civa-cattu, cata-cattu,caṭa-cattu eṉṟu kuṟikkappaṭṭaṉa.
   3. cātaṉa-iyal
   cārntataṉ vaṇṇamātal uyiriṉ ciṟappilakkaṇam. aḵtu acattākiya
   ulakattaiyum cattākiya param poruḷaiyum aṟiyavallatu; acattai viṭṭuc
   cattaip paṟṟakkūṭiyatu eṉpatu ēḻām cūttirattil viḷakkappaṭukiṟatu.
   uyirkaḷiṉ tavattāl iṟaivaṉ kuruparaṉ vaṭivaṅ koṇṭuvantu, pakkuvam
   uṭaiyārkku ñāṉattai uṇarttit taṉpāl avarkaḷaic cērppaṉ eṉpatai eṭṭām
   cūttiram viḷakkuvatākum; ñāṉam peṟṟavar ainteḻuttu ōti, ñāṉa nilaiyaik
   kākka vēṇṭum eṉpatu oṉpatām cūttirattiṟ kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.
   4. payaṉ-iyal
   ivvāṟu ñāṉattaip pēṇum -uyirkaḷ iṟaivaṉ tammuṭaṉ oṟṟittu niṟṟalāl pāca
   nīkkam peṟum eṉpatu pattām cūttirattuḷ viḷakkappaṭukiṟatu. iṟaivaṉ
   uyirkaṭkut tuṇaiyāka niṉṟu civappēṟu allatu mutti nilaiyaik kāṭṭuvāṉ,
   avai kāṇumāṟu utaviyum ceyvāṉ eṉpatu patiṉōrām cūttirattil
   kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. cīvaṉ muktarkaḷ malanīkkak karuttuṭaiyavarāy, aṭiyār
   iṇakkam uṭaiyavarāy, civa vēṭattaiyum civaṉ kōvilaiyum vaḻipaṭum
   niyamam uṭaiyavarāy niṟpar eṉpatu 12-ām cūttirattil viḷakkappaṭukiṟatu.
   4. civañāṉa cittiyār
   itaṉaiyum aṭuttuvarum irupā irupaḵtu eṉṉum nūlaiyum iyaṟṟiyavar
   meykaṇṭār mutal māṇavarākiya aruḷ-nanti-civācāriyar eṉpavar. "civañāṉa
   cittiyār" eṉṟum nūl civañāṉa pōtattiṉ vaḻinūl; parapakkam, cupakkam
   eṉṉum iraṇṭu perum pirivukaḷai uṭaiyatu. civañāṉa pōtattil avaiyaṭakkam
   kūṟiya orē pāṭṭiṉai aṭippaṭaiyākakkoṇṭu, "parapakkam" tōṉṟiyatu.
   "cupakkam", civañāṉa pōtattiṉ 12 cūttiraṅkaḷiṉ poruḷai 12 atikāraṅkaḷil
   virittu viḷakkuvatu.
   civa ākamap piramāṇaṅkaḷai oppukkoḷḷāta camayaṅkaḷ puṟaccamayaṅkaḷ
   eṉappaṭṭaṉa. vaitikac cārpillāta camayaṅkaḷ "puṟappuṟac camayaṅkaḷ”
   eṉavum. vaitikac cārpuṭaiyavai "puṟaccamayaṅkaḷ" eṉavum kūṟappaṭṭaṉa.
   puṟappuṟac camayikaḷuḷ ulakāyatar, cautrāntikar, yōkacārar, mātyamikar,
   vaipāṭikar ākiya nālvakaip pauttar, nikaṇṭavātikaḷ, ācīvakar eṉṉum
   iruvakaic camaṇar aṭaṅkuvar. paṭṭācāriyaṉ matam, pirapākara matam eṉṉum
   iruvakai mīmāmcaka matamum, cattap piramavātam-māyāvātam- pāṟkariya
   vātam-kirīṭap pirama vātam eṉṉum nālvakai ēkāṉma vātamum, pāñcarātrikam
   eṉṉum vaiṇava matamum puṟaccamayattuḷ aṭaṅkum. ivar aṉaivaruṭaiya
   camayak koḷkaikaḷaikkūṟi, cittāntattuṭaṉ muraṇvaṉavaṟṟai maṟuttuc
   cittāntattai nilai niṟuttuvatē "para pakkam" eṉpatu. itu 301
   ceyyuṭkaḷaik koṇṭatu.
   cupakkattil civañāṉa pōtap poruḷē viritturaikkappaṭutaliṉ, atu paṟṟiya
   viparam iṅkut tēvai illai. ippakuti 828 ceyyuṭkaḷ koṇṭatu. cittiyāriṉ
   perumaiyai, "civaṉukku mēl teyvamillai, cittikku viñciya nūlillai"
   eṉṉum paḻamoḻiyālum tāyumāṉavar pārāṭṭuraiyālum naṉkuṇaralām. [35]
   5. irupā-irupaḵtu
   itu, ñāṉa-kuruvākiya meykaṇṭārai muṉṉilaiyākki, viṉāvutal pōlavum, avar
   viṭai kūṟutal pōlavum, cittāntak karuttukaḷai 20 ceyyuṭkaḷil kūṟum nūl.
   āṇavattiṉ eṭṭu iyalpum, māyaiyiṉ ēḻu iyalpum, karmattiṉ āṟu iyalpum
   itaṉkaṇ kūṟappaṭṭuḷa. caivat tirumuṟaikaḷuḷ viḷaṅkum aruḷ toṭarkaḷākiya
   "kāṇpārār kaṇṇutalāy kāṭṭākkālē", "uṉṉiluṉṉum uṉṉāviṭil viṭṭiṭum" [36]
   eṉpavaṟṟukku viḷakkaṅkaḷ tarappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. "iru nilaṉāyt tīyāki" eṉṟu
   toṭaṅkum appar tiruttāṇṭakattiṟkum.[37] "āṭpā lavarkkaruḷum
   ātimāṇpum”38 eṉṟum tiruñāṉa campantar tiruppācurattiṟkum poruḷ viḷakkam
   pārāṭṭattakum muṟaiyiṟ ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷatu. icciṟunūl meykaṇṭāratu
   peruñciṟappiṉai aṟivikkum perumaiyuṭaiyatu.
   ------------
   [35]. Ennatkanni, Gurumarabin -Vanakkam', V.5.
       [36]. tēvāram, cāmināta paṇṭitar patippu, pak. 1244
       [37]. appar, pak. 510, ce. 138. campantar. pak. 511. ce. 4;2

   6. uṇmai viḷakkam
   itu meykaṇṭa tēvaratu maṟṟoru māṇavarāṉa tiruvatikai-maṉavācakam
   kaṭantār eṉpavar ceytatu; 53 veṇpākkaḷai uṭaiyatu; itu muppattāṟu
   tattuvaṅkaḷaiyum, [39] āṇavam iruviṉai eṉpavaṟṟiṉ iyalpaiyum, [40]
   āṉmāviṉ iyalpaiyum [41], kaṭavuḷiṉ iyalpaiyum, [42] ainteḻuttiṉ
   uṇmaiyaiyum, [43] tam āciriyariṭattut tamakku viḷakkumpaṭi vēṇṭa,
   āciriyar viṭaiyaḷittatupōlac ceyyappaṭṭa nūlākum. muttiyilum mūṉṟu
   poruḷākum iṟai, uyir, ulakam uṇṭu eṉṟu viḷakkum perumaiyuṭaiyatu iṉṉūl.
   [44]
   ------------
   [39].ce. 4-21, [40].ce. 22, [41]. ce. 23-26,
       [42]. ce. 27-29, [43]. ce. 30-44, [44]. ce. 50

   umāpati civācāriyār iyaṟṟiya nūlkaḷ
   1. civappirakācam
   itu civañāṉa pōtattiṟkuc cārpu nūlākum. itaṉ pāyirattuḷ tirukkayilāya
   paramparai āciriyarkaḷ iṉṉār eṉpatum, caiva nūlkaḷiṉ iyalpum, tīṭcai
   vakaikaḷum curukkamākavum viḷakkamākavum ōtappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. nūliṉ
   avaiyaṭakkattil nūlkaḷai ārāyntu uṇmai teriyum muṟai naṉku
   viḷakkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. paḻamai paṟṟi oṉṟai naṉṟeṉṟum, putumai paṟṟi oṉṟait
   tīteṉṟum koḷḷutal tavaṟu eṉpatu āciriyar karuttu.
   innūl nūṟu viruttaṅkaḷai uṭaiyatu. itil, caiva cittānta atvaita
   nilai-uṭalum uyirum, kaṇṇoḷiyum katiroḷiyum, uyiraṟivum kaṇṇoḷiyum pōla
   iraṇṭaṟak kalattal eṉṟu viḷakkam kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. āṇavam, kaṉmam,
   viṉaippayaṉ varum vaḻikaḷ, māyaiyiṉ pirivukaḷ naṉku viḷakkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
   pala camayattāriṉ mutti pētaṅkaḷ vakuttukkūṟi, avaṟṟiṟku mēlatāyc
   cittānta mutti viḷaṅkum muṟaimai uṇarttappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
   2. tiruvaruṭpayaṉ
   itu kuṟaḷ veṇpākkaḷāl āyatu; pattu atikāraṅkaḷai uṭaiyatu. ovvōr
   atikārattum pattuk kuṟaḷkaḷ uṇṭu. iṟaivaṉatu iyalpu kūṟum mutal
   atikāram "patimutunilai" eṉṉum peyaruṭaiyatu. ēṉai atikāraṅkaḷiṉ
   peyarum ivvāṟē avai kūṟum poruḷiṉ iyalpai viḷakkuvaṉa. avai uyiravai
   nilai, iruḷmala nilai, aruḷatu nilai, aruḷuṟu nilai, aṟiyuneṟi, uyir
   viḷakkam, iṉpuṟu nilai, ainteḻuttaruḷnilai, aṇaintōr taṉmai eṉpaṉa.
   3. viṉā veṇpā
   itu patiṉmūṉṟu pākkaḷāl ākiyatu. iḵtu āciriyar, tam kuruvākiya
   maṟaiñāṉacampantariṭam cāttira uṇmaikaḷaik kēṭṭut teḷiyum muṟaiyil
   amaintatu. iruḷum oḷiyum ōriṭattil kūṭal aritu eṉa vitantu, taṉpāl
   iṟaivaṉ niṉṟa viyappai aṟivikkum pāṭalum, kāṇpāṉ-kāṭci-kāṭṭappaṭumporuḷ
   eṉṉum mūvakai uṇarcciyai nīttavar mutti nilaiyai aṭaivar eṉak kuṟikkum
   tiruppāṭṭum nuṭpam ceṟintavai.
   4. pōṟṟip paḵṟoṭai veṇpā
   itu, uyir toḻiṟpaṭum muṟaimai uṇarttum vāyilākat tamakkuc civañāṉam
   nalkic civāṉupavam īnta kuruvaip paḵṟoṭai veṇpāvāl vāḻttiya nūlākum.
   iṟaivaṉatu paranilaiyum avaṉatu potunilaiyākum aintoḻil naṭattum
   tiṟamum, uyirkaḷai maṟaittuniṟkum āṇavattiṉ iyalpum, ataṉai oḻikkavēṇṭi
   iṟaivaṉ cērkkum māyai kāriyamākum uṭaṟkaruvikaḷ, pōkaṅkaḷ, ulakaṅkaḷ
   ivaṟṟiṉ pērutaviyum, uyirkaḷ karppavācattilum tuṉpuṟum tuṉpanilaikaḷum,
   piṟaku viṉaikku īṭāka nērum maṟumaip payaṉkaḷum, piṉṉaip piṟavikaḷil
   kūṭiya iruviṉai yoppu mutaliya pakkuva nilaikaḷum, iṟaivaṉ mūvakai
   uyirkaṭku aruḷum tiṟamum, uyirkaḷ civañāṉam peṟṟuc cariyai mutaliya
   neṟikaḷil oḻuki aṭainta civappēṟṟiṉ nilaiyum, "nāmē piramam" eṉpataṉ
   iḻivum, caivacittānta muttip perunilaiyum muṟaiyāka naṉku
   viḷakkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
   5. koṭikkavi
   itu mikac ciṟiya nūl; nāṉku veṇpākkaḷāl ākiyatu. oḷikkum iruḷukkum oṉṟē
   iṭam eṉṟa pakuti iṉpuṟattakkatu. ainteḻuttiṉ nuṭpam iṟuti veṇpāviṟ
   kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. itu tillaiyiṟ koṭiyēṟum poruṭṭup pāṭiya nūlātaliṉ,
   "koṭikkavi" eṉappeyar peṟṟatu.
   6. neñcu viṭu tūtu
   iḵtu āciriyar tamatu uḷḷattai maṟai ñāṉa campantarpāl mālai vāṅkumpaṭit
   tūtuviṭu mukattāl pala nūlkaḷiṉ poruḷ muṭipaiyum viḷakkuvatu;
   kaliveṇpāviṉāl āṉatu; 129 kaṇṇikaḷai uṭaiyatu. tacāṅkam viritturaikkum
   pakuti paṭittu iṉpuṟat takkatu. ñāṉāciriyaṉ pācanīkkam ceyyum
   tiṟattiṉai viḷakkum pakuti kuṟikkattakkatu.
   7. uṇmaineṟi viḷakkam
   itu tattuvarūpam, tattuvak kāṭci, tattuva catti, āṉma vaṭivam, āṉma
   taricaṉam, āṉma cutti, civarūpam, civataricaṉam, civayōkam, civa pōkam
   eṉṉum pattuk kāriyaṅkaḷai iṉitu viḷakkum nūl. it tacakāriyam paṟṟiya
   kuṟip pukkaḷ muṉ coṉṉa civappirakācattil uḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟiṉ virivē
   innūlākum.
   8. caṅkaṟpa nirākaraṇam
   itu akac camayaṅkaḷ palavaṟṟiṉ koḷkaikaḷaik kūṟi, avaṟṟaic cittāntap
   pārvaiyāl maṟukkum nūl. civañāṉa cittiyār-parapakkamum innūlum cittānta
   caivattiṉ vēṟāṉa camayak koḷkai vēṟupāṭukaḷai aṟitaṟku mikavum
   payaṉpaṭukiṉṟaṉa. nimitta kāraṇa pariṇāma vāti caṅkaṟpattil appar,
   campantartam tiruppatikaṅkaḷ, tiruvācakam, tiruvicaippā, kāraikkāl
   ammaiyār aṟputat tiruvantāti eṉpaṉa eṭuttāḷappaṭṭaṉa. caivacittānta
   nilaiyē pallāṟṟāṉum mikac ciṟantatu eṉpatai ic ciṟu nūl naṉku
   viḷakkukiṟatu.
   -------------------------

                        10. caiva camaya varalāṟu*

   * itu tiṇṭukkal aruḷ neṟit tirukkūṭṭattil (1-5-55) apirāmi ammaṉ
   kōviliṟ pēcappaṭṭatu.
   cintu veḷiyil
   civaneṟi allatu caiva camayam eṉpatai niṉaikkumpōtu civaneṟikkē uriya
   liṅka vaḻipāṭu niṉaiviṟku varukiṉṟatu. intiyāvilum ekiptilum mālṭṭā
   mutaliya tīvukaḷilum ciṟiyaṉavum periyaṉavumāṉa civaliṅkaṅkaḷ pala
   putai poruḷ ārāycciyil kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. intiyāvil mikappaḻaiya nākarika
   nakaraṅkaḷ eṉṟu ārāycciyāḷarkaḷāl kūṟappaṭum moheñcotarō, harappā
   mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil pala liṅkaṅkaḷ kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. harappāvil maṭṭum
   600-kku mēṟpaṭṭa ciṟu liṅkaṅkaḷ kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. paṇṭai makkaḷ icciṟiya
   liṅkaṅkaḷait tāyittukaḷ pōlak kaḻuttilō kaiyilō kaṭṭiyuruntirukkalām.
   cintu veḷiyil kiṭaitta oru muttiraiyil, mūṉṟu mukaṅkaḷaikkoṇṭa maṉita
   uruvam oṉṟu yōkattil amarntiruppatu pōṉṟu kāṇap paṭukiṉṟatu. ataṉ
   mārpil mukkōṇa vaṭivattil amainta patakkaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
   avvuruvattiṉ valappuṟam yāṉaiyum puliyum, iṭappuṟam erutum kāṇṭā
   mirukamum niṟkiṉṟaṉa. yōkiyiṉ pīṭattiṟku aṭiyil iraṇṭu māṉkaḷ
   irukkiṉṟaṉa. civapirāṉ yāṉait tōlaip pōrttavar, pulittōlai araiyil
   kaṭṭiyavar, erutiṉai ūrtiyākak koṇṭavar, māṉai ēntiyavar eṉṟa
   nūṟkaruttukkaḷai nōkka, yōkiyiṉ uruvam civaṉaik kuṟippatākalām eṉṟu
   kūṟutal poruttamākum. anta yōkiyiṉ talaimītu vaḷainta erumaik kompukaḷ
   irukkiṉṟaṉa. intiya aracāṅkap putaiporuḷ ārāyccit talaivarāṉa car jāṉ
   mārṣal, "ivvuruvam civaṉaik kuṟippatu" eṉṟu kūṟiyuḷḷār. yōkiyai aṭuttu
   vilaṅkukaḷ iruttal, civaṉ-pacupati eṉpataik kuṟikkiṉṟatu. mūṉṟu
   talaikaḷ muṉpu terivatāl piṉpaṟam iraṇṭu talaikaḷ iruttal kūṭum eṉṟu
   aṟiñar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.
   cintu veḷiyil vilaṅku vaṇakkamum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. nanti vaṇakkam
   ciṟappiṭam peṟṟateṉṟu kūṟalām. oru kōvil muṉ nanti niṟpatu oru
   muttiraiyil kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. cintuveḷi makkaḷ ciṟanta vaṇikarkaḷ.
   avarkaḷukku erutu mikavum payaṉpaṭṭatu. ataṉāl erutu vaṇakkattiṟkuriya
   poruḷāka māṟiyirukkalām. piṉṉar nāḷaṭaivil erutu vaṇakkam liṅka
   vaṇakkattōṭu iṇaintirukkalām eṉṟu ārāycciyāḷar karutukiṉṟaṉar. ivai
   aṉaittaiyum oṉṟupaṭuttip pārtta car jāṉ mārṣal eṉṟa aṟiñar "cintu
   veḷiyiṟ kiṭaitta putumaikaḷuḷ mutaliṭam peṟattakkatu caivattiṉ
   paḻamaiyēyākum. atu mākkal kālam allatu ataṉiṉum muṟpaṭṭa kālattiṟku
   nammai aḻaittuc celkiṟatu; iṉṟaḷavum vaḻakkil uḷḷa mikappaḻaiya
   camayamāka viḷaṅkukiṟatu" eṉṟu viyappum makiḻvum poṅkak kūṟiyuḷḷār.*
   -----
   * Among the many reveltions that Mohenjo-Daro and Harappa have had in
   store for us, none perhaps is more remarkable than this discovery that
   Saivisam has a history going back to the chalcolithic age or perhaps
   even further still, and it thus takes its place as the most ancient
   living faith in the worl." -Sir John Marshall in his preface to
   "Mohenjo -Daro and the Indus Civilization". vol.I. p. vii.
   caṅka kālattil civaṉ
   caṅka kālattil civaṉ-ālamar celvaṉ, mukkaṇṇaṉ, peruntēvaṉ mutaliya pala
   peyarkaḷāl vaḻipaṭappaṭṭāṉ. caṅka kālak kaṭavuḷar varicaiyil civaṉē
   mutaliṭam peṟṟu viḷaṅkiṉāṉ eṉpataic cilappatikāram, maṇimēkalai
   maturaik kāñci ivaṟṟāl aṟiyalām. muruka vaṇakkamum koṟṟavai vaḻipāṭum
   iruntaṉa.
   caṅka kālattai aṭutta mutaṟ pallavar kālattil kōpperuñcōḻaṉ ēṟattāḻa
   eṇpatu civaṉ kōvilkaḷaik kaṭṭiṉāṉ eṉṟu tēvāram kūṟukiṟatu.
   pakti neṟi
   vaṭa intiyāvil puttar kālam mutal kuptar āṭci varaiyil pautta camayamē
   celvākkuṭaiyatāyiruntatu. kuptarkaḷ caiva vaiṇava mataṅkaḷaip pōṟṟi
   vaḷarttaṉar. ātalāl akkālattil, atukāṟum pala nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷākak
   kūṟappaṭṭuvanta cevivaḻic ceytikaḷellām purāṇaṅkaḷāka vaṭamoḻiyil
   eḻutappaṭṭaṉa. cāti vēṟupāṭukaḷ vaṟpuṟuttappaṭṭaṉa. potu makkaḷuṭaiya
   uḷḷaṅkaḷaik kavaruvataṟkāka āṭal pāṭalkaḷaik koṇṭa paktineṟi ēṟpaṭṭatu.
   intap pakti neṟi teṉṉāṭṭilum paravattoṭaṅkiyatu. pācupatam, lakulīca
   pācupatam, kāpālikam, māviratam, cakti vaṇakkam, kumaraṉai vaḻipaṭum
   kaumāram mutaliyaṉa teṉṉāṭṭil paraviṉa. ivaiyaṉaittum caiva camayattiṉ
   uṭpirivukaḷākak koḷḷappaṭṭaṉa. ēṟattāḻak ki.pi. 400-500 kāla ellaikkuḷ
   ceyyappaṭṭatākak karutappaṭum tirumūlar tirumantirattilum ivai iṭam
   peṟṟaṉa.
   "viricaṭai antaṇar viratikaḷ pācupatar kāpālikar
       teruviṉil poliyum tiruvārūr ammāṉē"

   eṉṟu ki.pi. 7-ām nūṟṟāṇṭil vāḻnta apparum ivarkaḷaik kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār.
   aṟupattu mūṉṟu nāyaṉmāruḷ ippalavakaic caivarum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉar.
   ki.pi. 7-ām nūṟṟāṇṭumutal 9-ām nūṟṟāṇṭu varai ip paktineṟi tamiḻakattil
   peruveḷḷamākap paraviyatu. kōvilkaḷ mikap palavākat tōṉṟiṉa. āṭalum
   pāṭalum makkaḷ uḷḷaṅkaḷaik koḷḷai koṇṭaṉa. tikampara camaṇamum
   pauttamum nāṭṭil celvākkai iḻantaṉa. caivaraip piṉpaṟṟiyē vaiṇava
   āḻvārkaḷum paktineṟiyaip parappiṉar. pallava vēntarkaḷ caivattaiyum
   vaiṇavattaiyum tam iru kaṇkaḷākak karuti vaḷarttuvantaṉar. aḻintu
   viṭakkūṭiya maṇ, maram, ceṅkal, cuṇṇāmpu, ulōkam, ivaṟṟāl ākiya
   kōvilkaḷaik kaṭṭāmal, pallava maṉṉar malaiccarivukaḷil kuṭaivaraik
   kōvilkaḷai amaittārkaḷ; piṉpu pāṟaikaḷaiyē kōvilkaḷāka amaittārkaḷ.
   avai oṟṟaik kal kōvilkaḷ eṉappaṭum. avaṟṟiṉ piṉṉarē ceṅkaṟkaḷaip pōlak
   karuṅkaṟkaḷai uṭaittu avaṟṟaik koṇṭu cuvar eḻuppik kōvil kaṭṭat
   toṭaṅkiṉar. iṅṅaṉam amaikkappaṭṭa mutaṟkōvilē kāñci kayilācanātar
   kōvil. pallava maṉṉar ivvāṟu kōvilkaḷ amaippatilum ciṟpaṅkaḷaiyum
   ōviyaṅkaḷaiyum amaippatilum kōvil āṭciyilum karuttaic celuttiṉamaiyāl,
   caiva vaiṇava camayaṅkaḷ naṉku vaḷarcci peṟalāyiṉa. nāyaṉmārkaḷ
   nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa civaṉkōvilkaḷukkuc ceṉṟu patikaṅkaḷ pāṭiṉar. paṇṇōṭu
   pāṭappaṭṭa appāṭalkaḷ makkaḷ uḷḷaṅkaḷai iḻuttaṉa. akkālattil caivattil
   cātivēṟupāṭukaḷ kavaṉikkappaṭavillai.
   "āvurittut tiṉṟuḻalum pulaiya rēṉum kaṅkaivār
       caṭaikkarantārkku aṉparākil
       avar kaṇṭīr nām vaṇaṅkum kaṭavuḷārē"

   eṉṉum appar vākkai nōkkuka. iṭaiyiṭaiyē aṭiyāriṭam cāti vēṟupāṭṭuṇarcci
   kāṇappaṭṭatu; āṉāl aḵtu avvappoḻutu māṟṟappaṭṭatu eṉpatum nāyaṉmār
   varalāṟukaḷāl iṉituṇarappaṭum. cātiyaṟṟa camutāyam tāṉ oṉṟu paṭṭu vāḻa
   iyalum. camaṇa camayattilum pautta camayattilum cātikaḷ
   pēcappaṭuvatillai. ātalāl avaṟṟai oḻikkap puṟappaṭṭa caivamum
   vaiṇavamum cāti vēṟupāṭukaḷai mutalil kavaṉikkavillai. kurukkaḷ marapil
   piṟanta cuntarar uruttira kaṇikaiyāṉa paravaiyāraiyum, vēḷāḷap
   peṇmaṇiyākiya caṅkiliyāraiyum maṇantukoṇṭamaiyē ivvuṇmaiyai viḷakkap
   pōtiya cāṉṟākum.
   pauttacamaṇaṅkaḷ celvākkiḻantu caivam maṭṭum taṉiyaracuceluttiya
   piṟkālattil, cuntarar ceytukoṇṭa tirumaṇaṅkaḷ iraṇṭum muṉ viṉaip
   payaṉāl nērntavai eṉṟu katai kaṭṭi viṭappaṭṭatu. kayilāyattil
   cuntararmītu kātal koṇṭa iru mātarum nilavulakil caṅkiliyārākavum
   paravaiyārākavum piṟantaṉar eṉpatu akkatai. aṅṅaṉam piṟantavarkaḷ
   cuntarar tōṉṟiya kurukkaḷ marapilēyē piṟantirukkalām allavā? avarkaḷ ēṉ
   kaṇikaiyar marapilum vēḷāḷar marapilum piṟattal vēṇṭum? iv
   viṉākkaḷukkut takka viṭai kiṭaittalaritu. mēlum, kātalaic cuvaikkap
   piṟantavarākak kūṟap paṭṭavar kātalaip pōtiya aḷavu cuvaikkavillai eṉṟu
   periya purāṇamē kūṟukiṟatu.
   cōḻar āṭciyil
   cōḻar kālattil kōvilkaḷ perukiṉa; vaḻipāṭṭu muṟaikaḷ perukiṉa;
   vaḻivaḻic caivarāṉa cōḻa maṉṉar ellāk kōvilkaḷilum tirumuṟai
   ōtuvārkaḷai niyamikka ēṟpāṭu ceytaṉar. irāca rācaṉ kaṭṭiya tañcaip
   periya kōvilil maṭṭum paṇpaṭṭa ōtuvār 48 pēr amarttappaṭṭaṉar. āṭal
   pāṭalkaḷukkāka nāṉūṟu patiyilār amarttappaṭṭaṉar. ovvoru kōvililum
   viḻākkaḷ naṭaipeṟaṟaṉa; caiva camaya nūlkaḷ paṭittup potumakkaḷukku
   viḷakkap paṭṭaṉa. caiva cittānta cāttiraṅkaḷāṉa civañāṉa pōtam mutaliya
   nūlkaḷ tōṉṟiṉa.
   kōvilkaḷai aṭuttu maṭaṅkaḷ iruntu camayak kalviyai vaḷarttu vantaṉa.
   ammaṭaṅkaḷil yāttirikar uṇpikkappaṭṭaṉar. periya kōvilkaḷil caracuvati
   paṇṭāram eṉṟa nūl nilaiyaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa. periya kōvilkaḷil maruttuva
   maṉaikaḷum amaintiruntaṉa. avaṟṟil aṟuvai maruttuvarum (Surgeon), nōy
   maruttuvarum (Physician), tātimārum (Nurses) iruntaṉar. maruntu
   vakaikaḷaik koṇṭuvaruvōrum, avaikaḷaip pakkuvam ceyvōrum iruntaṉar.
   kōvil maṇṭapaṅkaḷil icai, naṭaṉam, nāṭakam mutaliya kalaikaḷ
   vaḷarkkappaṭṭaṉa. ciṟpa ōviyak kalaikaḷ kōvilkaḷil vaḷarcci peṟṟaṉa.
   kōvilukkuḷḷēyē ūrāṭci maṉṟamum naṭai peṟṟatu. ūril pañcam ēṟpaṭṭu nāṭṭu
   makkaḷ naliyumpoḻutu kōvilil uḷḷa poṉ veḷḷi nakaikaḷum pāttiraṅkaḷum
   urukki ūrārkkuk kaṭaṉākat tarappaṭṭatu eṉṟu ālaṅkuṭik kalveṭṭuk
   kūṟukiṉṟatu. ittakaiya muṟaikaḷāl kōvil aṟivu vaḷarccikkum, āṉma
   vaḷarccikkum, poruḷātāra vaḷarccikkum ēṟṟa nilaikkaḷaṉāy iruntu
   vantatu. aracaṉ mutal āṇṭi īṟāka aṉaivarum kōvilait tam uyirāka
   matittaṉar. kōvil ūr naṭuvilē amaintu makkaḷait taṉvayappaṭuttivantatu.
   camayattiṉ uyirnāṭi āṭṭam
   ivvāṟu caiva camayam nāḷoru mēṉiyum poḻutoru vaṇṇamumāka vaḷarntu
   vantatu. āṉāl caivattuḷ cāti vēṟupāṭukaḷ tōṉṟi vaḷarntu vayiram
   pāyntuviṭṭaṉa. camutāyattil pala cātikaḷukkup pala urimaikaḷ
   maṟukkappaṭṭaṉa. ceruppu aṇintukoṇṭu teruvil naṭakkac cila cātikaḷukku
   urimaiyillai. māṭivīṭu kaṭṭikkoḷḷac cila cātikaḷukku urimaiyillai. cila
   cātiyar icavvaḷavu tūrattiṉ niṉṟu pēcavēṇṭum, cilar ivvaḷavu tūrattil
   otuṅki niṟka vēṇṭum eṉṟa varaiyaṟaikaḷ ēṟpaṭṭaṉa. ikkoṭumaikaḷ ellām
   pala kōvil kalveṭṭukkaḷil kāṇalām. ikkoṭumaikaḷāl caiva camutāyam
   valimai kuṉṟiyatu. maṉa oṟṟumai akaṉṟatu.
