   intap paritāpa nilaiyil ki.pi. 14-ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ toṭakkattil mālik-kāpūr
   paṭaiyeṭupput teṉṉāṭṭil naṭantatu. pukaḻpeṟṟa mīṉāṭciyammaṉ kōvil
   karuvaṟai maṭṭum tappiyatu. eñciya kōpuraṅkaḷum tiruccuṟṟukkaḷum
   taraimaṭṭamākkappaṭṭaṉa. peruṅkōvilkaḷil irunta nakaikaḷum piṟavum
   koṇṭu cellappaṭṭaṉa. nāṭāṇṭa pāṇṭiyaṉ, makkaḷai naṭṭāṟṟil viṭṭu
   ōṭiviṭṭāṉ. cātikkoṭumaiyāl iḻivupaṭuttappaṭṭa makkaḷ taṉmāṉa uṇarvuṭaṉ
   paṭaiyeṭuttavarai etirttu niṟka muṭiyavillai. pala kōvilkaḷ
   tākkappaṭṭaṉa. vikkirakaṅkaḷiṉ kaikālkaḷ oṭikkappaṭṭaṉa. ūrkaḷ
   koḷḷaiyaṭikkappaṭṭaṉa.
   caivarum vaiṇavarum paṭaiyeṭuttavarkkup paṇivu kāṭṭiṉar. "pōr"
   eṉṟavuṭaṉ tuḷḷikkutitta caṅkakālat tamiḻaṉ - cāti aṟiyāta tamiḻaṉ.
   mālikkāpūr paṭaiyeṭuttapōtu vāḻnta tamiḻaṉ - cātik kaṭṭuppāṭṭāl
   nacukkappaṭṭa tamiḻaṉ. mūṭak koḷkaikaḷālum cāti veṟiyiṉālum camaya
   veṟiyiṉālum caiva camayam taṉ āṟṟalai iḻantu etirikkup paṇintatu. caiva
   camaya varalāṟṟil itu iraṅkattakkap pakutiyākum. pallavar kālac
   caivarkaḷ camaṇaraik kaḻuvēṟṟiṉar; piṟa camayattāraik
   koṭumaippaṭuttiṉar. cōḻarkālac caivar vaiṇavarōṭu oṉṟupaṭṭu vāḻa
   maṟuttaṉar; irāmānucaraic cōḻa nāṭṭiliruntē viraṭṭiṉar. ikkoṭumaikaḷiṉ
   viḷaivaittāṉ ayalār paṭaiyeṭuppāl caivarkaḷ anupavittaṉar. "muṟpakal
   ceyyiṉ piṟpakal viḷaiyum" eṉpatu caiva varalāṟṟil uṇmaiyākiviṭṭatu.
   camaya māṟṟam
   caiva camayattil cāti eṉṉum peyarāl iḻivu paṭuttappaṭṭa makkaḷ, muslim
   āṭci nilaiyāṉatum, intu camayattil taṅkaḷukku ēṟpaṭṭa iḻiviṉāl, cāti
   aṟiyāta islāttait taḻuviṉar; vēṟu cilar taṅkaḷukkuk kalviyum maruttuva
   utaviyum maṉita urimaikaḷaiyum utaviya kiṟittuva camayattaic cārntaṉar.
   kiṟittuva camayamum cātiyaṟiyāc camayam. 'caivarkaḷ' eṉṟa peyaruṭaṉ
   pāmararāyum vilaṅkukaḷāyum vāḻkkai naṭattiya tamiḻarkaḷ, kiṟittuva
   camayattait taḻuvik kalvi kaṟṟu vāḻkkaiyil muṉṉēṟalāyiṉar. tām
   ceytuvanta koṭumaikaḷāl tamatu camutāyam valuviḻantatu. makkaḷ matam
   māṟiṉar eṉpaṉa aṟintum, camutāyac cīraḻiviṟkum camayac cīraḻivukkum tām
   kāṭṭiya cātivēṟupāṭukaḷē kāraṇam eṉpatai aṟintum, akkālattu uyar makkaḷ
   cuyanala veṟiyiṉāl cātikaḷai oḻikka muṉ varavillai. "camayattil nāṅkaḷ
   talaiyiṭa māṭṭōm" eṉṟa āṅkilēyar vākkuṟuti ivvuyarntavar koṭumaikaḷukku
   araṇ ceyvatu pōl amaintatu. mēṉmēlum cātiveṟi talai tūkkiyatu. kōviliṉ
   karuvaṟaiyil iṉṉa vakuppār iruntu vaḻipaṭalām, karuvaṟaikku veḷiyē iṉṉa
   vakuppār niṉṟu vaḻipaṭalām, kōvilukkuḷḷē iṉṉa vakuppār nuḻaiyak kūṭātu
   eṉṟa kaṭṭuppāṭukaḷ vaḷarntaṉa.
   muṟpōkku
   āṅkilakkalvi nāṭeṅkum paravat toṭaṅkiyatu. intiyāvait tavira vēṟu enta
   nāṭṭilum cātikaḷ illai eṉpataip paṭittavar uṇarntaṉar; cātikaḷ
   iṟaivaṉāl uṇṭākkappaṭṭavai eṉṟu tamakku mēlōr kūṟivanta kūṟṟu muḻup poy
   eṉpatai uṇarntaṉar. avvuṇarcciyē namatu camutāya maṟumalarccikku
   aṭippaṭai. nāṭeṅkum ivvuṇarcci vaḷarat talaippaṭṭatu. kāntiyaṭikaḷ cāti
   oḻippu iyakkattil tīviramāka īṭupaṭṭār. tāḻttappaṭṭa makkaḷukku maṉita
   urimaiyai aḷittār, avarkaḷukkum kōvil nuḻaivu kiṭaittatu. iṅṅaṉam
   periyōr palar muyaṟciyiṉ payaṉāka, iṉṟu kōviluḷ cāti vēṟupāṭu kāṇpatu
   eṉpatu niṟuttappaṭṭatu. āyiṉum camayam cīrtiruntiyatā?
   camayattil pāci
   iṭittal, maḻai peytal mutaliya iyaṟkai nikaḻccikaḷait teyvaṅkaḷiṉ
   ceyalkaḷākap paṇṭai makkaḷ niṉaittaṉar; tam maṉam pōṉavāṟu avaṟṟiṟkuk
   kataikaḷ kaṭṭiṉar. akkataikaḷ nāṭaḷaṭaivil camayattiṟkuriya kataikaḷāka
   māṟiviṭṭaṉa. innāṭṭavar ekiptu, cumēriyā mutaliya nāṭukaḷōṭu kaṭal
   vāṇikattil toṭarpu koṇṭa kāraṇattāl, avvannāṭṭuk kataikaḷ cila iṅkum
   parava vaḻiyēṟpaṭṭatu. innāṭṭuk kataikaḷ annāṭukaḷiṟ parava vaḻi
   ēṟpaṭṭatu. itaṉāl cila kataikaḷ peyaraḷavil vēṟupaṭṭum poruḷaḷavil
   oṉṟupaṭṭum immūṉṟu nāṭukaḷilum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ittakaiya pala
   kataikaḷ itikācaṅkaḷilum purāṇaṅkaḷilum iṭam peṟṟuviṭṭaṉa. ivaṟṟiṉ
   payaṉāka, "aṉpē civam" eṉpatai aṭippaṭaiyākak koṇṭa caiva camayam,
   paurāṇika camayamāka māṟiviṭṭatu. immāṟutal nāyaṉmār kālattil
   toṭaṅkiyatu; āyiṉum kālappōkkil pala kataikaḷ camayattuṟaiyil
   cērkkappaṭṭu, navakkiraka vaḻipāṭu pōṉṟa putiya teyva vaḻipāṭukaḷ
   iṇaikkappaṭṭu, paurāṇika camayam vaḷarcci peṟṟatu. pāmara makkaḷ
   mikuntuḷḷa innāṭṭil ikkataikaḷē camayattiṉ uyirnāṭiyākak karutap
   paṭukiṉṟaṉa.[1]
   ----------
   "In Babylonia, as elsewhere, the priests utilized the floating material
   from which all mythologies were framed and impressed upon it their
   doctrimes."-Myths of Babylonia and Assyia, p, 189.
   ivaṟṟaic collip piḻaippavarum palarāki viṭṭaṉar. itaṉāl "aṉpē civam"
   eṉṟa koḷkai pātāḷattiṟ putaikkappaṭṭu, ataṉ mītu aṟivukkup poruttamaṟṟa
   kataikaḷ kaṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. ikkēṭṭiṉāl, perumpālāṉa caiva makkaḷ tirunīṟu
   aṇivatōṭu amaintaṉar. tīya paḻakkavaḻakkaṅkaḷaik kaiviṭṭilar. camaya
   varalāṟum aṟintilar. tirunīṟṟai aṇintu koṇṭē aṭāta ceykaikaḷaic ceyyum
   caivarkaḷ perukiviṭṭaṉar. tam vīṭukaḷil oru maṇi nēramutal mūṉṟu maṇi
   nēram civapūjai ceyyum cilar irukkiṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷil palar
   oḻukkamaṟṟavarkaḷākavum tīya vaḻikaḷil poruḷīṭṭupavarākavum, āṉāl kōvil
   tiruppaṇi ceypavarkaḷākavum kāṇkiṉṟaṉar. camayap pōrvaiyil palar
   ceykiṉṟa tīya ceyalkaḷukku aḷavillai. oru caivaṉ piṟa caivaṉiṭam aṉpu
   kāṭṭa vēṇṭumeṉpataiyum maṉitat taṉmaiyōṭu naṭantukoḷḷavēṇṭum
   eṉpataiyumē caivar maṟantuviṭṭaṉar. kalvic cerukkālum celvac cerukkālum
   patavic cerukkālum caivar palar tam camayattaic cērnta piṟaruṭaṉ
   paḻakuvatum illai; matippatum illai. ittakaiya pala kāraṇaṅkaḷāl
   camutāyattil caivar oṟṟumaiyiṉṟi valimai kuṉṟiviṭṭaṉar.
   caiva maṭaṅkaḷ
   caivamaṭaṅkaḷ caiva camayattai vaḷarppataṟkeṉṟē ēṟpaṭṭavai. nāṭāṇṭa
   maṉṉarum piṟarum innaṉṉōkkattiṟkē celvattaiyum pala vēli nilaṅkaḷaiyum
   immaṭaṅkaḷukku utaviṉar; pala kōvilkaḷai immaṭaṅkaḷiṉ āṭciyil viṭṭu
   vaittaṉar; aṟivilum oḻukkattilum camaya cāttirak kalviyilum mutirnta
   perumakkaḷai maṭattut talaivarkaḷāka vaittaṉar. piṟkālattil in nilai
   māṟiyatu. maṭātipati jamīṉai āṭci puriyum jamīṉtāraip pōla
   maṭattukkuriya varavu celavukaḷilum vaḻakkukaḷilum perum poḻutaip
   pōkkat toṭaṅkiṉar. oru cilar oḻukkam tavaṟiyum naṭakkattalaippaṭṭaṉar.
   eṉavē, kāla vēṟupāṭṭālum maṭaṅkaḷiṉ nilai vēṟupāṭṭālum araciyal
   māṟṟattālum caivacamaya piraccāram illāmaiyālum potumakkaḷukkum caiva
   maṭaṅkaḷukkum toṭarpu ēṟpaṭavillai. potu makkaḷukkup poruḷ viḷaṅkāta
   nilaiyil cila nūlkaḷai acciṭṭu veḷippaṭuttalum, paṭitta oru cilarukkup
   poṉṉāṭai pōrttalum, āṇṭu viḻākkaḷil camayac coṟpoḻivu ceyvōrukkuc
   ciṟitu poruḷ koṭuppatumē maṭaṅkaḷ ceyyum caivap paṇikaḷāka
   iruntuvantaṉa. iṉṟu inta nilai māṟi, ivaṟṟōṭu potu makkaḷukakup
   paḷḷikkūṭam vaittal, maruttuva nilaiyam amaittal, tirukkuṟaḷ pōṉṟa
   ariya nūlkaḷai veḷiyiṭutal mutaliya toṇṭukaḷil maṭaṅkaḷ iṟaṅkiyuḷḷamai
   varavēṟkattakkatu,
   maṭaṅkaḷ ceyya vēṇṭuvaṉa
   1.potu makkaḷukkup puriyum vakaiyil- eḷiya vakaiyil caivacamaya
   varalāṟu, caiva camayak koḷkaikaḷ, caivam vāḻkkaiyōṭu toṭarpu koṇṭuḷḷa
   taṉmai, tirukkōvilkaḷiṉ ciṟappu, avaṟṟai makkaḷ payaṉpaṭuttum muṟai,
   caiva cittāntattiṉ teḷivāṉa karuttu- ivaṟṟaic ciṟuciṟu nūlkaḷāka
   veḷiyiṭṭup potu makkaḷukku ilavacamāka vaḻaṅka vēṇṭum.
   2. caiva camayattil cāti vēṟupāṭu illai eṉpatai vaṟpuṟuttic caṭṭam
   koṇṭuvarappaṭal vēṇṭum. ellōrum orē pantiyil uṭkārntu uṇavu koḷḷum
   muṟai kaiyāḷappaṭutal vēṇṭum. cāti oḻippuc caṭṭam varum varaiyiṟ
   caivarkkuḷ kalappumaṇam vaḷartal vēṇṭum.
   3. pala ciṟṟūrkaḷil toṭakka nilaippaḷḷikaḷait tōṟṟuvittuc caivap
   piḷḷaikaṭku ilavacak kalvi aḷikkavēṇṭum. appaḷḷikaḷil ciṟuvarkku camaya
   aṟivu kaṟpikkappaṭal vēṇṭum. acciṟṟūrkaḷil maruttuva maṉaikaḷ,
   paṭippakaṅkaḷ, nūl nilaiyaṅkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟai amaittup potumakkaḷukkum,
   maṭattukkum toṭarpu uṇṭākkutal vēṇṭum. ēḻāyiram mailkaḷukkappāliruntu
   innāṭṭukku vanta kiṟuttuvap pātirimārkaḷ ivaṟṟaic ceytamaiyāṟṟāṉ taṅkaḷ
   camayattai vaḷarttaṉar eṉṟa uṇmaiyai nām uṇartal vēṇṭum.
   4. āṅkila aṟivum, tamiḻ aṟivum peṟṟa camayap piracākarai niyamittu
   ovvoru maṭamum makkaḷiṭaic camayappiracāram ceyvittalvēṇṭum.
   vīṇāṉavaiyum vāḻkkaikkup payaṉ paṭātaṉavumāṉa kataikaḷaic colli
   makkaḷai mēlum mūṭarkaḷākkāmal camaya neṟiyil vāḻa vaḻi amaittut
   tarutal vēṇṭum.
   5. "paḻaiyaṉa kaḻitalum putiyaṉa pukutalum vaḻuvala" eṉṟa muṟai paṟṟi
   potumakkaḷukkuc ciṟitum payaṉtarāta viḻākkaḷaiyum āravāraṅkaḷaiyum
   oḻittu, avaṟṟiṟkuc celavākum paṇattai ēḻaic caiva makkaḷukku utavi,
   avarkaḷaic civaneṟiyil vāḻvikka vaḻi ceytal vēṇṭum. makkaḷai
   vāḻvikkattakum muṟaiyil "civapirāṉ toḻiṟcāla" pōṉṟa kaṭavuṭ peyarkaḷāl
   toḻiṟcālaikaḷai niṟuvavēṇṭum.
   ivai pōṉṟa muyaṟcikaḷil maṭaṅkaḷ īṭupaṭṭāl, caivam taḻaittōṅka
   vaḻiyuṇṭu.
   tamiḻil vaḻipāṭu
   tamiḻar varippaṇattālum tamiḻ vēntar muyaṟciyālum uṇṭākkappaṭṭa
   kōvilkaḷē it tamiḻnāṭṭil iruppavai. ivaṟṟil caivat tirumuṟaikaḷ ōtiyē
   vaḻipāṭu ceyyappaṭṭu vantatu. ciṟanta civattalaṅkaḷāṉa tirumaṟaikkāṭu
   "vētāraṇyam" eṉavum, tiruveṇkāṭu "cuvētāraṇyam" eṉavum, tirumutu kuṉṟam
   "viruttācalam" eṉavum māṟiya kālattil, tamiḻil irunta vaḻipāṭu
   vaṭamoḻiyil māṟiviṭṭatu pōlum! cuvāmi, ammaṉ peyarkaḷ ellām tamiḻ
   makkaḷukkup puriyāta apīta kujāmpāḷ mutaliya vaṭamoḻip peyarkaḷāka
   māṟiviṭṭaṉa. inta alaṅkōla nilai, kōvilukkuc cellum potumakkaḷiṉ
   uḷḷattil aḻuntiya pakti ēṟpaṭātu ceytuviṭṭatu,. vaḻipaṭa varum
   makkaḷukkup puriyāta moḻiyil arccaṉai ceyyappaṭumāyiṉ, ataṉāl
   vaḻipaṭuvōr uḷḷam evvāṟu kuḻaiyum? caivam cīraḻivataṟkum, kōvil
   vaḻipāṭu kuṟaintu varuvataṟkum itu mikacciṟanta kāraṇamākum.
   aṟanilaiyap pātukāppāḷarkaḷum, maṭaṅkaḷiṉ talaivarkaḷum, caivap
   perumakkaḷum ittuṟaiyil uṭaṉē kavaṉam celuttutal vēṇṭum. aṟivum
   ārāycciyum kēḷvi kēṭkum tiṟaṉum perukivarum ikkālattil, aṟivukkup
   porunta naṭappatē camayavaḷarccikku ēṟṟatu.
   potu makkaḷ toṇṭu
   caiva makkaḷ taṅkaḷ illaṅkaḷil piṟakkum kuḻantaikaḷukkuc caivap
   peyarkaḷ vaippatu nallatu. civaneṟic celvaṉ, tēvārac celvaṉ, neṭumāṟaṉ,
   kōṭpuli, kaṇṇappaṉ, kaḻaṟciṅkaṉ eṉṟāṟpōla nāyaṉmār peyarkaḷaiyum
   tirumuṟaikaḷil varum arum peyarkaḷaiyum vaittu vaḻaṅkutal paḻamaiyaip
   putuppittu nilaikkacceyvatākum. ivvāṟē peṇ piḷḷaikaḷukku
   maṅkaiyarkkaraci, civa araci, veṇkāṭṭu naṅkai, cantaṉa naṅkai mutaliya
   caivap peyarkaḷai vaikkalām.
   caivar tam ūrkaḷil ēṟpaṭum putiya terukkaḷukkuc cēkkiḻār teru,
   campantar teru, ālāla cuntarar teru, eṉac caivap peyarkaḷai vaikka
   ēṟpāṭu ceyyalām. ivvāṟē tam vaḷamaṉaikaḷukkuc 'cēkkiḻār akam' 'cuntarar
   akam' eṉap peyarkaḷaic cūṭṭalām.
   civaneṟic celvarum caiva caṅkaṅkaḷum caiva camaya varalāṟu, caivap
   peyarkaḷ, pārāyaṇattiṟkuriya patikaṅkaḷ pōṉṟavaṟṟaic ciṟunūlkaḷaka
   eḻutac ceytu ilavacamākavō, kuṟainta vilaiyilō parapputal vēṇṭum;
   aṭikkaṭi takkāraik koṇṭu, ikkālattiṟku ēṟṟa muṟaiyil, coṟpoḻivukaḷiṉ
   vāyilākac caiva camayap paṟṟai ūṭṭutal vēṇṭum; vāḻkkaikkum
   camayattiṟkum uḷḷa toṭarpai terivittalē mikka payaṉuṭaiya coṟpoḻivākum.
   ---------------------------------------
   caiva camayam muṟṟiṟṟu
